# AoE2 DE AI 脚本命令全量（386 条）

来源：<https://airef.github.io/commands/commands-details.html>（AoE2 AI Scripting Encyclopedia，数据文件 `js/commands.js` 2.0 MB）

抓取：2026-11-08，node vm 执行站点 JS 后导出 115 个数组，字段与站内一致。

字段说明：`类型` = Fact / Action / Command / Other；`版本` = 自 AoC 起可用的版本；`参数` 保留站内原始 token。
示例代码块已把站点里的 `{title,data}` 结构还原为「说明 + 可复制代码」。

## 按类型统计
| 类型 | 条数 |
|---|---|
| Action | 187 |
| Fact | 162 |
| Fact/Action | 21 |
| Other | 16 |

> `Fact/Action` 是两类都能用的命令（如 `up-timer-status`）。


---

## Action（187）

### `acknowledge-event`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Scenarios · 复杂度 Medium*

**Acknowledges a received event by resetting the associated flag.**

Acknowledges a received event by resetting the associated flag. Scenario triggers that execute an AI Script Goal effect are the only events that AI scripts can detect.

This command, along with [event-detected](/commands/commands-details.html#event-detected), is used to detect an AI Script Goal effect from a scenario trigger, often with the intention of changing the AI behavior after the scenario trigger has fired. The scenario designer chooses an AI Trigger number for the AI Script Goal effect in the scenario editor. Then, the event-detected command in the AI script will detect when this trigger effect happens. The event-detected command will remain true after the AI Script Goal trigger effect fires, so acknowledge-event is used to reset the event-detected flag so that event-detected will no longer be true, similar to how the [disable-timer](/commands/commands-details.html#disable-timer) command clears a timer that has triggered or how the [acknowledge-taunt](/commands/commands-details.html#acknowledge-taunt) command accepts the taunt message.

**参数**：`EventType` — `EventId`

*示例：If an AI Script Goal trigger effect has fired with AI trigger 36, detect it with event-detected and acknowledge it with acknowledge-event. gl-trigger-event-fired is set to 1 so that (goal gl-trigger-event-fired 1) can be used as a condition to change the AI behavior in the scenario after the AI Script Goal trigger effect has fired.*
```
(defconst gl-trigger-effect-fired 101)
(defrule
	(event-detected trigger 36)
=>
	(set-goal gl-trigger-effect-fired 1)
	(acknowledge-event trigger 36)
)
```

相关命令：`event-detected`, `set-signal`, `up-get-event`, `up-get-signal`, `up-set-event`, `up-set-signal`

### `acknowledge-taunt`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Chat, Debugging, Other Player Info · 复杂度 Low*

**Acknowledges the taunt (resets the flag).**

Acknowledges the taunt (resets the flag). Like other event systems in the AI, taunt detection requests explicit acknowledgement.

In simple terms, whenever an AI receives a taunt message, [taunt-detected](/commands/commands-details.html#taunt-detected) will remain true for the given taunt until the taunt is acknowledged. If the taunt is not acknowledged, your AI's response to the taunt will happen repeatedly.

The action allows "focus-player", "target-player", and "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It also allows the use of rule variables for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber), such as "this-any-ally" or "this-any-enemy". It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `TauntId`

*示例：Launch an attack when an ally taunts 31 ("Attack an Enemy Now!") if the AI has enough soldiers to attack. Acknowledge the taunt so that the attack command is only sent once.*
```
(defrule
	(taunt-detected any-ally 31)
	(military-population >= 8)
=>
	(attack-now)
	(acknowledge-taunt this-any-ally 31)
)
```

相关命令：`taunt`, `taunt-detected`, `taunt-using-range`

### `attack-now`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Attack · 复杂度 Low*

**Forces attack with currently available attack units.**

Forces attack with currently available attack units. Units are designated as attack units by using [sn-percent-attack-soldiers](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-percent-attack-soldiers) or [sn-percent-attack-boats](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-percent-attack-boats).

Once the attack-now command is issued, all attack units will group up into formations, pick a building or unit to target, and march toward that location to attack, mostly ignoring enemy units and buildings along the way. Once the soldiers reach the target, they will attack the target and nearby units. Once the target is destroyed, a new target will be selected. This process will continue until all attack units from the attack-now command are killed.

To stop an attack-now attack, use [up-reset-attack-now](/commands/commands-details.html#up-reset-attack-now).

Three important notes:
Do not spam this command! Frequent attack-now commands can cause severe lag. Each successive attack-now command will cause the units to recalculate their target, which can often be quite expensive and should not be done frequently. If possible, use a timer to issue an attack-now command every X seconds instead.attack-now is only one of four methods to attack the enemy: attack-now, attack groups, town size attack (TSA), and DUC. The Attack groups method sends an attack when [sn-number-attack-groups](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-number-attack-groups) is greater than zero. TSA sends an attack when the enemy buildings are within the distance of [sn-maximum-town-size](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-maximum-town-size). Direct Unit Control (guides here: [Link](https://airef.github.io/resources/res-index.html)) can manually issue direct attack or patrol actions to selected groups of units against manually selected targets.As noted above, sn-number-attack-groups is used in the attack groups attack method and does not impact attack-now.

*示例：Issue an attack every 5 minutes (300 seconds) if we have > 12 military units.*
```
(defconst t-attack-timer 1)
(defrule
	(game-time >= 300)
	(up-timer-status t-attack-timer != timer-running)
	(military-population > 12)
=>
	(attack-now)
	(enable-timer t-attack-timer 300)
)
```

相关命令：`up-reset-attack-now`, `up-retreat-now`, `up-retreat-to`, `up-set-offense-priority`

相关 SN：`sn-attack-group-gather-spacing`, `sn-attack-intelligence`, `sn-attack-winning-player`, `sn-attack-winning-player-factor`, `sn-enable-offensive-priority`, `sn-group-form-distance`, `sn-percent-attack-soldiers`, `sn-percent-attack-boats`, `sn-target-player-number`

### `build`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings · 复杂度 Low*

**Builds the given building.**

Builds the given building if the building is available to the player and the building can be constructed without escrowed resources. If you want to construct walls or gates, use the corresponding [build-wall](/commands/commands-details.html#build-wall), [build-gate](/commands/commands-details.html#build-gate), or [up-build-line](/commands/commands-details.html#up-build-line) commands instead.

The Action allows the use of building line wildcard parameters for [BuildingId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#BuildingId). The only wildcard parameter available is watch-tower-line. However, it is better to use watch-tower instead of watch-tower-line, even after Guard Tower or Keep upgrades due to some bugs with watch-tower-line. Simply using (build watch-tower) will work regardless of tower upgrades. Building classes cannot be used with this command.

Important Note: Always use a [can-build](/commands/commands-details.html#can-build) or [up-can-build](/commands/commands-details.html#up-can-build) condition in every rule where you use the build command. Without this condition, the building queue for this building may get stuck for the rest of the game.

When this command is issued, the AI engine will add the specified building to the building placement queue. If [sn-enable-new-building-system](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-enable-new-building-system) is set to 0, the engine will only add the building to the placement queue if there isn't already a building of the same type being constructed or waiting to be placed, but if the SN is set to 1 this check is removed, and an unlimited number of buildings of the same type are allowed to be queued for placement or be constructed at once. You can limit the number of buildings added to the placement queue with a [up-pending-objects](/commands/commands-details.html#up-pending-objects) condition.

At the end of each script pass, the AI engine checks if the AI has explored the minimum percentage of the map required by [sn-initial-exploration-required](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-initial-exploration-required). If so, it will attempt to place each building that is currently in the placement queue. If the building was added to the queue with the (build) command, the AI will place most buildings at a random location within [sn-maximum-town-size](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-maximum-town-size) tiles from the main town center using whatever value sn-maximum-town-size is set to at the end of the script. [sn-minimum-town-size](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-minimum-town-size) has no effect on building placement except for towers. However, four tiles around the TC are reserved around every town center for farms, and all buildings are placed at least one tile apart. For a complete list on the min and max distances where each building can be built, see this list here: [link](https://airef.github.io/resources/articles/helpful-info.html#building-construction-distances).

Buildings placed with this command will avoid the following locations:
Ally (and self): will avoid placing the building on tiles where an allied building already exists.Enemy: will avoid placing the building on tiles where an enemy building already exists. Will also avoid placing a building within the attack range of a tower, TC, or castle, + 0.5 tiles.

There are many other commands that you can use instead of this command that you more precise control over building placement, such as [build-forward](/commands/commands-details.html#build-forward), [up-build](/commands/commands-details.html#up-build), and [up-build-line](/commands/commands-details.html#up-build-line).
Placement Exceptions

Several buildings have variations on how they are placed that are different from the description above:

Town Centers are placed like most buildings when [sn-town-center-placement](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-town-center-placement) is set to the default value of 0. However, if sn-town-center-placement is set to the [BuildingId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#BuildingId) of another building, such as "mill" or "lumber-camp", the town center will follow the placement rules of that building instead.

Mills and Folwarks are not placed in a random location within sn-maximum-town-size, but instead are built by a food resource within [sn-mill-max-distance](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-mill-max-distance). The AI engine by default prefers to build mills and folwarks by forage, then by shore fish, then by deer. However, this preference can be changed with [sn-preferred-mill-placement](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-preferred-mill-placement). Also, mills and folwarks are placed one tile away from food resource piles unless [sn-allow-adjacent-dropsites](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-allow-adjacent-dropsites) is set to 1 by the end of the script pass, and they are placed a minimum number of tiles from all dropsites (not just mills and folwarks), as specified by [sn-dropsite-separation-distance](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-dropsite-separation-distance).

Mining Camps are not placed in a random location within sn-maximum-town-size, but instead are built by a gold or stone resource within [sn-mining-camp-max-distance](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-mining-camp-max-distance) (or [sn-camp-max-distance](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-camp-max-distance) if sn-mining-camp-max-distance is set to 0), and they are placed at least 7 tiles from the main town center. If the closest gold mine distance to a dropoff point is greater than [sn-gold-dropsite-distance](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-gold-dropsite-distance), then it will prefer to place the mining camp near gold mines. Then it checks if the closest stone mine distance to a dropoff point is greater than [sn-stone-dropsite-distance](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-stone-dropsite-distance), and if it is, it will prefer to place the mining camp near stone. If neither condition is met, it prefers neither gold nor stone, and the mining camp placement behavior is undefined. It's possible the mining camp isn't placed, but this is untested. Also, mining camps are placed one tile away from gold and stone resource piles unless [sn-allow-adjacent-dropsites](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-allow-adjacent-dropsites) is set to 1 by the end of the script pass, and they are placed a minimum number of tiles from all dropsites (not just mining camps), as specified by [sn-dropsite-separation-distance](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-dropsite-separation-distance).

Lumber Camps are not placed in a random location within sn-maximum-town-size, but instead are built by a tree within [sn-lumber-camp-max-distance](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-lumber-camp-max-distance) (or [sn-camp-max-distance](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-camp-max-distance) if sn-lumber-camp-max-distance is set to 0), and they are placed at least 7 tiles from the main town center. Also, lumber camps are placed one tile away from trees unless [sn-allow-adjacent-dropsites](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-allow-adjacent-dropsites) is set to 1 by the end of the script pass, though even with the SN set to 1 the AI will sometimes fail to build the lumber camp adjacent to trees, and they are placed a minimum number of tiles from all dropsites (not just lumber camps), as specified by [sn-dropsite-separation-distance](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-dropsite-separation-distance). Usually the AI favors building lumber camps near forests rather than straggler trees, but the AI will build lumber camps near straggler trees if sn-lumber-camp-max-distance is to small for the AI to find an available forest to build the lumber camp by.

Docks are of course only placed on water, and there are several SNs that can affect their placement, such as [sn-dock-avoidance-factor](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-dock-avoidance-factor), [sn-dock-placement-mode](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-dock-placement-mode), [sn-dock-proximity-factor](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-dock-proximity-factor), and [sn-minimum-water-body-size-for-dock](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-minimum-water-body-size-for-dock).

Farms are automatically placed near town centers, mills, and folwarks. The AI engine prefers to place farms around TCs instead of mills or folwarks, but it will place farms around mills or folwarks if all spaces immediately next to the town center are already filled with farms.

Fish Traps should not be placed with the build command. Instead, they should only be placed with up-build-line. It's possible they can be placed with the build command, but they often won't be placed in the right location. Also, make sure to use (up-assign-builders c: fish-trap c: -1) to make sure that villagers aren't sent to contruct them.

Outposts, at least according to this [info](https://airef.github.io/resources/articles/helpful-info.html#building-construction-distances), are placed outside the town, at a distance between sn-maximum-town-size and twice the distance of sn-maximum-town-size. They might also have a preference to be placed on hills like towers do (see the towers section below). If you choose to build outposts, make sure you test to make sure you like their placement location. You can build them in more precise locations or inside the town if you use the place-control or place-point options with up-build, or you can also place them with up-build-line.

Towers are the only type of building that uses sn-minimum-town-size as the minimum distance they can be placed from the starting town center. By default they have a preference to be placed on hills, but you can remove this preference by setting [sn-ignore-tower-elevation](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-ignore-tower-elevation) to 1. This preference for hills is not used for castles or kreposts.

Donjons. Everything from towers applies to donjons. To construct donjons with serjeants, set [sn-allow-serjeant-building](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-allow-serjeant-building) to 1.

Gates cannot be placed with the build command. Construct them with the [build-gate](/commands/commands-details.html#build-gate) or up-build-line command. To build palisade gates, set [sn-gate-type-for-wall](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-gate-type-for-wall) to 1 before using the build-gate command.

Trebuchets. Yes, (build trebuchet) actually works. Every scripter should try it once in their life. However, soon you'll see why it's considered cheating.

**参数**：`BuildingId`

*示例：Order the AI to construct a house at a random location within sn-maximum-town-size.*
```
(defrule
	(can-build house)
=>
	(build house)
)
```

相关命令：`build-forward`, `build-gate`, `build-wall`, `can-build`, `can-build-with-escrow`, `up-build`, `up-build-line`, `up-can-build`, `up-can-build-line`

相关 SN：`sn-allow-adjacent-dropsites`, `sn-allow-serjeant-building`, `sn-camp-max-distance`, `sn-dropsite-separation-distance`, `sn-food-dropsite-distance`, `sn-gold-dropsite-distance`, `sn-ignore-tower-elevation`, `sn-enable-new-building-system`, `sn-initial-exploration-required`, `sn-lumber-camp-max-distance`, `sn-maximum-town-size`, `sn-mill-max-distance`, `sn-minimum-town-size`, `sn-mining-camp-max-distance`, `sn-mule-cart-dropsite-placement`, `sn-preferred-mill-placement`, `sn-preferred-storage-pit-placement`, `sn-random-placement-factor`, `sn-stone-dropsite-distance`, `sn-town-center-placement`, `sn-unexplored-construction`, `sn-wood-dropsite-distance`

### `build-forward`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings · 复杂度 Low*

**Builds the given building close to an enemy.**

Builds the given building close to an enemy if the building is available to the player and the building can be constructed without escrowed resources.

The Action allows the use of building line wildcard parameters for [BuildingId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#BuildingId). The only wildcard parameter available is watch-tower-line. However, it is better to use watch-tower instead of watch-tower-line, even after Guard Tower or Keep upgrades due to some bugs with watch-tower-line. Simply using (build watch-tower) will work regardless of tower upgrades. Building classes cannot be used with this command.

Important Note: Always use a [can-build](/commands/commands-details.html#can-build) or [up-can-build](/commands/commands-details.html#up-can-build) condition in every rule where you use the build-forward command. Without this condition, the building queue for this building may get stuck for the rest of the game.

When this command is issued, the AI engine will add the specified building to the building placement queue. If [sn-enable-new-building-system](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-enable-new-building-system) is set to 0, the engine will only add the building to the placement queue if there isn't already a building of the same type being constructed or waiting to be placed, but if the SN is set to 1 this check is removed, and an unlimited number of buildings of the same type are allowed to be queued for placement or be constructed at once.

At the end of each script pass, the AI engine checks if the AI has explored the minimum percentage of the map required by [sn-initial-exploration-required](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-initial-exploration-required). If so, it will attempt to place each building that is currently in the placement queue. If the building was added to the queue with the build-forward command, the AI will place the building near the enemy player specified by [sn-target-player-number](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-target-player-number) or the player specified by [sn-attack-winning-player](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-attack-winning-player) if sn-target-player-number is set to 0.

Buildings placed with build-forward will avoid placing the building on tiles where an enemy building already exists, and it will also avoid placing a building within any enemy building's line of sight, + 2 tiles.

**参数**：`BuildingId`

*示例：Order the AI to construct a watch tower near the enemy.*
```
(defrule
	(can-build watch-tower)
=>
	(build-forward watch-tower)
)
```

相关命令：`build`, `build-gate`, `build-wall`, `can-build`, `can-build-with-escrow`, `up-build`, `up-build-line`, `up-can-build`, `up-can-build-line`

相关 SN：`sn-allow-serjeant-building`, `sn-attack-winning-player`, `sn-ignore-tower-elevation`, `sn-enable-new-building-system`, `sn-initial-exploration-required`, `sn-max-skips-per-attempt`, `sn-number-build-attempts-before-skip`, `sn-target-player-number`, `sn-unexplored-construction`

### `build-gate`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Walls & Gates · 复杂度 Low*

**Builds a gate as part of the given perimeter wall.**

Builds a gate as part of the given perimeter wall if the gate is available to the player and the gate can be constructed without escrowed resources. The given perimeter must first be enabled with [enable-wall-placement](/commands/commands-details.html#enable-wall-placement). Perimeter 1 is usually between 10 and 20 tiles from the starting Town Center. Perimeter 2 is usually between 18 and 30 tiles from the starting Town Center. If wall placement is enabled at a particular perimeter with enable-wall-placement, the AI engine will attempt to plan a roughly circular wall pattern within the given perimeter distances when the [build-wall](/commands/commands-details.html#build-wall) command is issued. Once the AI finds an appropriate location to build a gate within the given perimeter and the build-gate command is issued, the AI will replace four wall segments with a gate foundation.

This command cannot be used to build a gate within wall segments that existed at the start of the game, such as the starting walls in Arena or Fortress.

In the DE version you can build palisade gates by setting [sn-gate-type-for-wall](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-gate-type-for-wall) to 1 before using this command.

**参数**：`Perimeter`

*示例：Order the AI to build a gate at perimeter 2.*
```
(defrule
	(can-build-gate 2)
=>
	(build-gate 2)
)
```

相关命令：`build`, `build-forward`, `build-wall`, `can-build-gate`, `can-build-gate-with-escrow`, `enable-wall-placement`, `up-build-line`, `up-can-build-line`

相关 SN：`sn-unexplored-construction`, `sn-gate-type-for-wall`

### `build-wall`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Walls & Gates · 复杂度 Low*

**Builds a wall line of the given wall type at the given perimeter.**

Builds a wall line of the given wall type at the given perimeter if the wall type is available to the player and the wall can be constructed without escrowed resources. The given perimeter must first be enabled with [enable-wall-placement](/commands/commands-details.html#enable-wall-placement).

The Action allows the use of wall line wildcard parameters for [WallId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#WallId). The only wall line wildcard parameter is stone-wall-line.

Perimeter 1 is usually between 10 and 20 tiles from the starting Town Center. Perimeter 2 is usually between 18 and 30 tiles from the starting Town Center. If wall placement is enabled at a particular perimeter with enable-wall-placement, the AI engine will attempt to plan a roughly circular wall pattern within the given perimeter distances and construct the wall according to this pattern. This command cannot be used to rebuild parts of wall segments that existed at the start of the game, such as the starting walls in Arena or Fortress.

**参数**：`Perimeter` — `WallId`

*示例：Order the AI to build a stone wall around the town at perimeter 2.*
```
(defrule
	(can-build-wall 2 stone-wall-line)
	(can-afford-complete-wall 2 stone-wall-line)
	(wall-completed-percentage 2 < 100)
=>
	(build-wall 2 stone-wall-line)
)
```

相关命令：`build`, `build-forward`, `build-gate`, `can-build-gate`, `can-build-gate-with-escrow`, `enable-wall-placement`, `up-build-line`, `up-can-build-line`

相关 SN：`sn-unexplored-construction`

### `buy-commodity`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Economy, Trading · 复杂度 Low*

**Buys one lot of the given commodity.**

Buys one lot of the given commodity. The AI will buy 100 of the given commodity (wood, food, or stone) at the current trading price.

**参数**：`Commodity`

*示例：Buy 100 food if the AI has enough gold to do so.*
```
(defrule
	(can-buy-commodity food)
=>
	(buy-commodity food)
)
```

相关命令：`can-buy-commodity`, `can-sell-commodity`, `commodity-buying-price`, `commodity-selling-price`, `sell-commodity`, `up-buy-commodity`, `up-sell-commodity`

### `cc-add-resource`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Cheat, Economy · 复杂度 Low*

**A cheating action that adds the given resource amount to the computer player.**

A cheating action that adds the given resource amount to the computer player. This command works even if cheats are disabled. It is to be used in scenarios to avoid late game oddities such as computer player villagers going all over the map while looking for the last pile of gold. Negative amounts can be used to remove resources from the computer player's stockpile.

**参数**：`Resource` — `Value`

*示例：If cheats are enabled, give an extra 1500 food to the computer player if it has less than 1000 food.*
```
(defrule
	(cheats-enabled)
	(food-amount < 1000)
=>
	(cc-add-resource food 1500)
)
```

相关命令：`cheats-enabled`, `up-cc-add-resource`, `up-cc-send-cheat`

相关 SN：`sn-add-starting-resource-food`, `sn-add-starting-resource-gold`, `sn-add-starting-resource-stone`, `sn-add-starting-resource-wood`

### `chat-local`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Chat, Debugging · 复杂度 Low*

**Displays the given string as a local chat message to all players.**

Displays the given string (a message in quotation marks) as a local chat message to all players. Local chat messages display chat messages in white rather than with the AI's player color, making this command strictly inferior to [chat-to-all](/commands/commands-details.html#chat-to-all).

If the chat message string starts with numerals, that number will be sent as a taunt to all players and the starting numerals will be removed from the message. For example, "1 TC" will send taunt 1 to all players and send the message " TC" to all players.

**参数**：`String`

*示例：Chat "glhf" to all players with a white text color.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(chat-local "glhf")
)
```

相关命令：`chat-local-to-self`, `chat-local-using-id`, `chat-local-using-range`, `chat-to-all`, `up-chat-data-to-all`

### `chat-local-to-self`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Chat, Debugging · 复杂度 Low*

**Displays a given string as local chat message to self.**

Displays a given string (a message in quotation marks) as local chat message. The message is displayed only if the user is the same player as the computer player sending the message. For debugging purposes only. Local chat messages display chat messages in white rather than with the AI's player color, making this command strictly inferior to [chat-to-player](/commands/commands-details.html#chat-to-player) with my-player-number as the player Id.

If the chat message string starts with numerals, that number will be sent as a taunt to self and the starting numerals will be removed from the message. For example, "1 TC" will send taunt 1 to self and send the message " TC" to self.

**参数**：`String`

*示例：Chat "testing" to self with a white text color.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(chat-local-to-self "testing")
)
```

相关命令：`chat-local`, `chat-local-using-id`, `chat-local-using-range`, `chat-trace`, `up-chat-data-to-self`

)

### `chat-to-player`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Chat, Debugging · 复杂度 Low*

**Sends a given string as a chat message to a given player.**

Sends a given string as a chat message to a given player.

If the chat message string starts with numerals, that number will be sent as a taunt to the specified player and the starting numerals will be removed from the message. For example, "1 TC" will send taunt 1 to the specified player and send the message " TC" to the specified player.

The fact allows "my-player-number", "focus-player", "target-player", and "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1). It also allows the use of rule variables for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber), such as "this-any-ally" or "this-any-enemy".

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `String`

*示例：If an enemy player is Goths, send a chat to this player with the AI's player color.*
```
(defrule
	(players-civ any-enemy gothic)
=>
	(chat-to-player this-any-enemy "I know you are a Goth")
)
```

*示例：Chat "testing" to self with the AI's player color.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(chat-to-player my-player-number "testing")
)
```

相关命令：`chat-local-to-self`, `chat-to-player-using-id`, `chat-to-player-using-range`, `taunt`, `up-chat-data-to-player`

It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1). It also allows the use of rule variables for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber), such as "this-any-ally" or "this-any-enemy".

Displays the given value to all players as a chat message, with "Trace " in front. Used purely for testing to check when a rule gets executed.

### `clear-tribute-memory`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Diplomacy · 复杂度 Low*

**Clears the given player's tribute memory.**

Clears the given player's tribute memory, the amount of a given resource received in tribute from the given player since the tribute memory was cleared. Only tribute memory for the given resource type is cleared. This command is used in conjunction with [players-tribute-memory](/commands/commands-details.html#players-tribute-memory), which allows you to check the amount of tribute received from the specified player since clear-tribute-memory was issued.

The action allows "focus-player", "target-player", and "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It also allows the use of rule variables for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber), such as "this-any-ally" or "this-any-enemy". It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `Resource`

*示例：If the AI has received 500 food from a human player, clear the food tribute memory from this player and change diplomatic stance toward the human player to ally.*
```
(defrule
	(players-tribute-memory any-human food >= 500)
=>
	(clear-tribute-memory this-any-human food)
	(set-stance this-any-human ally)
)
```

相关命令：`players-tribute`, `players-tribute-memory`

### `delete-building`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings · 复杂度 Low*

**Deletes exactly one building of a given type.**

Deletes exactly one building of a given type. You cannot use building classes with this command.

There are several other commands available to delete objects:

[delete-unit](/commands/commands-details.html#delete-unit): delete exactly one unit of a given type[up-delete-distant-farms](/commands/commands-details.html#up-delete-distant-farms): delete farms that are beyond a specified distance from a dropsite[up-delete-idle-units](/commands/commands-details.html#up-delete-idle-units): delete all idle units of the specified type[up-delete-objects](/commands/commands-details.html#up-delete-objects): delete all objects of the specified type that have less than the specified hitpoints[up-target-objects](/commands/commands-details.html#up-target-objects) or [up-target-point](/commands/commands-details.html#up-target-point): when used with the action-delete action, this command will delete all objects in the local search list

**参数**：`BuildingId`

*示例：If no allies are left in the game and the AI has a market, delete one market.*
```
(defrule
	(not	(player-in-game any-ally))
	(building-type-count market > 0)
=>
	(delete-building market)
)
```

相关命令：`delete-unit`, `up-delete-distant-farms`, `up-delete-idle-units`, `up-delete-objects`, `up-target-objects`, `up-target-point`

相关 SN：`sn-percent-building-cancellation`

### `delete-unit`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Units · 复杂度 Low*

**Deletes exactly one unit of a given type.**

Deletes exactly one unit of a given type. You cannot use unit classes with this command. my-unique-unit, my-elite-unique-unit, and my-unique-unit-line can also be used, which will automatically get the UnitId of the unique unit, elite unique unit, or unique unit line that the AI's civ can train from the castle.

There are several other commands available to delete objects:

[delete-building](/commands/commands-details.html#delete-building): delete exactly one building of a given type[up-delete-distant-farms](/commands/commands-details.html#up-delete-distant-farms): delete farms that are beyond a specified distance from a dropsite[up-delete-idle-units](/commands/commands-details.html#up-delete-idle-units): delete all idle units of the specified type[up-delete-objects](/commands/commands-details.html#up-delete-objects): delete all objects of the given type that have less than the specified hitpoints[up-target-objects](/commands/commands-details.html#up-target-objects) or [up-target-point](/commands/commands-details.html#up-target-point): when used with the action-delete action, this command will delete all objects in the local search list

**参数**：`UnitId`

*示例：If no allies are left in the game and the AI has trade carts, delete one trade cart.*
```
(defrule
	(not	(player-in-game any-ally))
	(unit-type-count trade-cart > 0)
=>
	(delete-unit trade-cart)
)
```

相关命令：`delete-building`, `up-delete-distant-farms`, `up-delete-idle-units`, `up-delete-objects`, `up-target-objects`, `up-target-point`

### `disable-rule`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Don't Use · 复杂度 Don't Use*

**Disables the given rule id. (Not Fully Implemented! Do Not Use!)**

**参数**：`Value`

相关命令：`enable-rule`

### `disable-self`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Other · 复杂度 Low*

**Disables the rule that it is part of so that the rule is never run again.**

Disables the rule that it is part of so that the rule is never run again. Since disabling takes effect in the next execution pass, other actions in the same rule are still executed once. Use this whenever you only want the rule to run once and never again. Rules disabled with a disable-self command are never read again, but they are still counted as rules by commands that jump over rules like [up-jump-rule](/commands/commands-details.html#up-jump-rule) or [up-jump-dynamic](/commands/commands-details.html#up-jump-dynamic).

*示例：Send a "glhf" message at the beginning of the game and disable the rule so that it won't send the message a second time.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(chat-to-all "glhf")
	(disable-self)
)
```

相关命令：`do-nothing`, `up-jump-direct`, `up-jump-dynamic`, `up-jump-rule`

### `disable-timer`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Timers · 复杂度 Low*

**Disables the given timer.**

Disables the given timer. The given timer can be any valid timer number, which can range from 1 to 100 (1-50 in UP and 1-10 in 1.0c). You can also substitute a defconst that is defined with a value between 1 and 100 if you want to give the timer a name.

Timers have three possible states, and they cannot have multiple states at once: timer-running, timer-triggered, and timer-disabled. disable-timer or [up-set-timer](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-timer) with a -1 timer length puts the timer in the timer-disabled state. [enable-timer](/commands/commands-details.html#enable-timer) or [up-set-timer](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-timer) with a timer length > 0 puts the timer in the timer-running state. disable-timer doesn't have to be used before using an enable-timer command.

**参数**：`TimerId`

*示例：If timer #3 is in the timer-triggered state, disable timer #3.*
```
(defrule
	(timer-triggered 3)
=>
	(disable-timer 3)
)
```

相关命令：`enable-timer`, `timer-triggered`, `up-get-timer`, `up-set-timer`, `up-timer-status`

### `do-nothing`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Other · 复杂度 Low*

**Does nothing. Used as a placeholder action if you don't want a rule to have any actions.**

Does nothing. Used as a placeholder action if you don't want a rule to have any actions. Every rule must have at least one fact and one action. In rare cases where you don't want to include any actions in your rule, use do-nothing as a placeholder to fulfill the one action requirement. One of these rare cases is when you want to temporarily comment out all the actions in your rule for testing purposes but you want to keep the facts section of your rule. Unlike [disable-self](/commands/commands-details.html#disable-self) do-nothing will not stop the rule from being checked each pass.

*示例：The chat-to-all command has been commented out. Use do-nothing to fulfill the one action minimum per rule requirement.*
```
(defrule
	(current-age == imperial-age)
=>
	;(chat-to-all "I have reached the Imperial Age.")
	(do-nothing)
)
```

*示例：Technically this counts as a valid rule. Do you agree?*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`disable-self`

### `enable-rule`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Don't Use · 复杂度 Don't Use*

**Enables the given rule id. (Not Fully Implemented! Do Not Use!)**

**参数**：`Value`

相关命令：`disable-rule`

### `enable-timer`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Timers · 复杂度 Low*

**Enables the given timer and sets it to the given time interval.**

Enables the given timer and sets it to the given time interval. The given timer can be any valid timer number, which can range from 1 to 100 (1-50 in UP and 1-10 in 1.0c). You can also substitute a defconst that is defined with a value between 1 and 100 if you want to give the timer a name.

Time intervals are measured in game time seconds, so enabling a timer for 240 seconds would start a 4 minute timer. If played on 2.0 speed (Fast speed), this 4 minute timer would last 2 minutes in real time.

Timers have three possible states, and they cannot have multiple states at once: timer-running, timer-triggered, and timer-disabled. [disable-timer](/commands/commands-details.html#disable-timer) or [up-set-timer](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-timer) with a -1 timer length puts the timer in the timer-disabled state. enable-timer or [up-set-timer](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-timer) with a timer length > 0 puts the timer in the timer-running state. disable-timer doesn't have to be used before using an enable-timer command.

**参数**：`TimerId` — `Value`

*示例：If we have 15 military population and timer #2 isn't running (either it is timer-triggered or timer-disabled), attack and enable the timer for 5 minutes.*
```
(defrule
	(military-population >= 15)
	(up-timer-status 2 != timer-running)
=>
	(attack-now)
	(enable-timer 2 300)
)
```

相关命令：`disable-timer`, `enable-timer`, `timer-triggered`, `up-get-timer`, `up-set-timer`, `up-timer-status`

### `enable-wall-placement`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Walls & Gates · 复杂度 Low*

**Enables wall placement for the given perimeter.**

Enables wall placement for the given perimeter, either perimeter 1 or perimeter 2. Walls cannot be built with the [build-wall](/commands/commands-details.html#build-wall) command at the given perimeter unless this command is used. Enabled wall placement causes the rest of the placement code to do some planning and place all structures at least one tile away from the future wall lines. If you are planning to build a wall, you have to explicitly define which perimeter wall you plan to use when the game starts. This is a one-time action and should be used during the initial setup.

Perimeter 1 is usually between 10 and 20 tiles from the starting Town Center. Perimeter 2 is usually between 18 and 30 tiles from the starting Town Center.

**参数**：`Perimeter`

*示例：Enable wall placement for perimeter 2.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(enable-wall-placement 2)
	(disable-self)
)
```

相关命令：`can-build-wall`, `can-build-wall-with-escrow`, `can-build-gate`, `can-build-gate-with-escrow`, `build-wall`, `build-gate`

### `fe-break-point`
*版本 DE · 分类 Debugging · 复杂度 Very High*

**DE only. Add a break point to force the AI debugger interface to display.**

DE only. Add a break point to force the AI debugger interface to display if the break point conditions are met. The break point conditions are met if the comparison between the first and second values is true and either the last parameter is -1 or the goal specified in the last parameter is set to a value >= 1. The debugger shows you various information about the AI's current state, such as the current value of each goal and the object IDs stored in the local and remote lists. Once the debugger is opened, you'll be able to step through your rules.

To enable the debugger you must first enable AI debugging for the game in the Steam launch options. Before launching the game, go to Steam => Right click game => Properties => in bottom box type AIDEBUGGING.

**参数**：`Value` — `compareOp` — `Value` — `OptionGoalId`

*示例：Pause the game and start the debugger when taunt 255 is sent. The break point is always issued because 1 is always equal to 1 and the OptionGoalId parameter is set to -1.*
```
(defrule
	(taunt-detected any-computer 255)
=>
	(fe-break-point 1 == 1 -1)
	(acknowledge-taunt this-any-computer 255)
)
```

*示例：Divide gl-temp by gl-divisor. If gl-divisor is equal to zero and gl-break-state is >= 1, catch a divide by zero error by starting the debugger. As a side note, the game will not crash if you divide by zero. The [up-modify-goal](/commands/commands-details.html#up-modify-goal) command should simply silently fail to do the division.*
```
(defconst gl-temp 101)
(defconst gl-divisor 102)
(defconst gl-break-state 103)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(set-goal gl-temp 100)
	(set-goal gl-divisor 0)
	(set-goal gl-break-state 1)
	(disable-self)
)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(fe-break-point 0 g:== gl-divisor gl-break-state)
	(up-modify-goal gl-temp g:/ gl-divisor)
)
```

### `fe-cc-effect-amount`
*版本 DE · 分类 Cheat, Scenarios · 复杂度 Very High*

**DE only. Apply a research-style effect with an integer value for the AI player.**

DE only. Apply a research-style effect with an integer value for the AI player. This is considered a cheat command, but cheats do not have to be enabled. When modifying objects, you may need to target ALL hidden variations, one-by-one, as well. Please consider in-game object upgrades, so that an upgrade will not push a unit's max hitpoints over 32768 or the object will be destroyed. If you disable an object with this command, in-game techs/ages (unless disabled) may re-enable them. The civ tech tree may also override changes.

This command can only use integer values. If you need to make an effect with a decimal value, use [fe-cc-effect-percent](/commands/commands-details.html#fe-cc-effect-percent).

**参数**：`EffectId` — `ItemId` — `AttrId` — `Value`

*示例：Give villagers 10 extra hitpoints.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(fe-cc-effect-amount effect-add-attribute villager-class attribute-hp 10)
)
```

相关命令：`fe-cc-effect-percent`

### `fe-cc-effect-percent`
*版本 DE · 分类 Cheat, Scenarios · 复杂度 Very High*

**DE only. Apply a research-style effect with a percentage for the AI player.**

DE only. Apply a research-style effect as a percentage for the AI player. This command is identical to [fe-cc-effect-amount](/commands/commands-details.html#fe-cc-effect-amount), except the value is divided by 100 to provide decimal precision. This is considered a cheat command, but cheats do not have to be enabled. When modifying objects, you may need to target ALL hidden variations, one-by-one, as well. Please consider in-game object upgrades, so that an upgrade will not push a unit's max hitpoints over 32768 or the object will be destroyed. If you disable an object with this command, in-game techs/ages (unless disabled) may re-enable them. The civ tech tree may also override changes.

This command can only use integer values.

**参数**：`EffectId` — `ItemId` — `AttrId` — `Percent`

相关命令：`fe-cc-effect-amount`

### `fe-exclude-from-attack-group`
*版本 DE · 分类 Attack · 复杂度 Low*

**DE only. Removes the given unit type from attack-now and attack-groups attacks.**

DE only. Removes the given unit type from attack-now and attack-groups attacks. To reset the list of units excluded from [attack-now](/commands/commands-details.html#attack-now) and attack-group attacks, use [fe-reset-attack-group-exclusion-list](/commands/commands-details.html#fe-reset-attack-group-exclusion-list).

**参数**：`typeOp` — `UnitId`

*示例：Exclude Cao Cao from attacks.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(fe-exclude-from-attack-group c: 1954) ;1954 = Cao Cao
	(disable-self)
)
```

相关命令：`fe-reset-attack-group-exclusion-list`

### `fe-filter-garrisoned`
*版本 DE · 分类 DUC · 复杂度 Very High*

**DE only. Filters whether garrisoned and/or ungarrisoned units are found in DUC searches.**

DE only. Filters whether garrisoned and/or ungarrisoned units are found in DUC searches. Set to 0 before a DUC search to exclude objects that are garrisoned in a building, ram, or transport ship from future DUC searches, but allow units that aren't garrisoned to be found (the default setting). Set to 1 before a DUC search to allow both garrisoned and ungarrisoned units to be found. Set to 2 before a DUC search to exclude ungarrisoned units.

Using [up-full-reset-search](/commands/commands-details.html#up-full-reset-search) or [up-reset-filters](/commands/commands-details.html#up-reset-filters) will reset the filter back to its default setting (0).

**参数**：`typeOp` — `Option`

*示例：Find up to 15 garrisoned villagers. Setting fe-filter-garrison to 2 will ensure that only garrisoned villagers are found.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-full-reset-search)
	(fe-filter-garrisoned c: 2)
	(up-find-local c: villager-class c: 15)
)
```

### `fe-reset-attack-group-exclusion-list`
*版本 DE · 分类 Attack · 复杂度 Low*

**DE only. Clears the list of unit types excluded from attack-now and attack-groups attacks.**

DE only. Clears the list of unit types excluded from attack-now and attack-groups attacks. To add unit types that should be excluded from [attack-now](/commands/commands-details.html#attack-now) and attack-group attacks, use [fe-exclude-from-attack-group](/commands/commands-details.html#fe-exclude-from-attack-group).

*示例：Clear the list of unit types excluded from attack-now and attack-groups attacks.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(fe-reset-attack-group-exclusion-list)
	(disable-self)
)
```

相关命令：`fe-reset-attack-group-exclusion-list`

### `fe-set-signal`
*版本 DE · 分类 Scenarios · 复杂度 Medium*

**DE only. Set the value of a multiplayer scenario trigger signal.**

DE only. Set the value of a multiplayer scenario trigger signal.

This action only works with a "Multiplayer AI Signal" trigger condition in a single and multiplayer scenario. For the "AI Signal" condition use [up-set-signal](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-signal) (only works in a single player scenario).

**参数**：`typeOp` — `SignalId` — `typeOp` — `Value`

*示例：Set the value of signal id 1 to 0.*
```
(defconst gl-value 100)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(fe-set-signal c: 1 c: 0)
)
```

相关命令：`acknowledge-event`, `event-detected`, `set-signal`, `up-get-event`, `up-get-signal`, `up-set-event`, `up-set-signal`

### `generate-random-number`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Other · 复杂度 Low*

**Generates a player-specific integer random number within given range.**

Generates a player-specific integer random number within given range (1 to [Value](/parameters/parameters-details.html#Value)). The number is stored internally and its value can be tested with [random-number](/commands/commands-details.html#random-number). Subsequent executions of this action generate new random numbers that replace existing ones.

If you want to store the random number in a goal, use [up-get-fact](/commands/commands-details.html#up-get-fact).

Unfortunately, the numbers generated by this command are not truly random, and restarting the game can sometimes result in the same random numbers being generated. It's best to avoid generating random numbers in the first few seconds of the game since the results can be less reliable.

If you want to generate a number that is more random, consider using [up-get-precise-time](/commands/commands-details.html#up-get-precise-time) to get a timestamp into a goal (such as gl-random-number) and then use (up-modify-goal gl-random-number c:mod X) where X is the range of values you want your random number to use. The mod operator (c:mod) divides the goal by the given value (X) and stores the remainder left over from the division. Thus, if X is 100, gl-random-number will range somewhere between 0 and 99. In addition to using up-get-precise-time, you can also do the same c:mod calculation with another number that is fairly random, like a player's score. Unfortunately, these alternative methods can only generate one random number per pass because game state information like up-get-precise-time and player scores are only updated between passes.

Important note: using any value less than 1 will cause the game to crash.

**参数**：`Value`

*示例：Generate a random number between 1 and 100 and store the value in a goal. This random number can be checked later with the random-number command or by using [up-compare-goal](/commands/commands-details.html#up-compare-goal) to compare gl-random-number with something else, such as (up-compare-goal gl-random-number > 50).*
```
(defconst gl-random-number 101)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(generate-random-number 100)
	(up-get-fact random-number 0 gl-random-number)
)
```

相关命令：`random-number`

### `log`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Debugging · 复杂度 Medium*

**Writes the given string to a log file. Only works in DE.**

Writes the given string to a log file. Used purely for testing purposes. Works only if logging is enabled. Logging is disabled in AoC (the old CD version of the game) and Userpatch. Use [up-log-data](/commands/commands-details.html#up-log-data) instead.

However, logging can be enabled in DE. To do this, you need to launch the game with the parameters LOGSYSTEMS=AIScript and VERBOSELOGGING (case sensitive). To do this with the Steam version, open your Steam games library with the Steam client, right click on Age of Empires II: Definitive Edition in the left sidebar that lists the games you own, and click Properties. In the Properties window, under the General tab, type the parameters above separated by spaces. Then, when you launch the game these parameters will be active.

The log produced in DE will be found in the Steam user folder, usually something like "C:\Users\[user ID]\Games\Age of Empires 2 DE\logs" but note that this log isn't just used by the AI (it would be best to log something identifying the AI log at the start of the game), some of these logs with VERBOSELOGGING can get quite large so it might be a good idea to periodically clean out the folder.

**参数**：`String`

*示例：Every time the AI attacks, write the string "Attack Launched" to the log file.*
```
(defconst t-attack-timer 1)
(defrule
	(defend-soldier-count >= 12)
	(timer-triggered t-attack-timer)
=>
	(enable-timer t-attack-timer 300)
	(attack-now)
	(log "Attack Launched")
)
```

相关命令：`up-log-data`

### `log-trace`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Debugging · 复杂度 Medium*

**Writes the given value to a log file. Doesn't work.**

Writes the given value to a log file. Used purely for testing to check when a rule gets executed. Works only if logging is enabled (which it isn't). Use [up-log-data](/commands/commands-details.html#up-log-data) instead. You can also use [log](/commands/commands-details.html#log) to log a text string if you are scripting for DE.

**参数**：`Value`

*示例：Log the value 99. Note: this command currently doesn't work. This example is purely for documentation purposes.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(log-trace 99)
	(disable-self)
)
```

相关命令：`log`, `up-log-data`

### `release-escrow`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Economy · 复杂度 Medium*

**Releases the computer player's escrow for a given resource type (sets the amount to 0).**

Releases the computer player's escrow for a given resource type (transfers all of the given resource type from its escrow stockpile into its normal stockpile, setting the amount stored in that resource's escrow stockpile to 0).

AIs can store each of their four resource stockpiles in one of two stockpile types: normal and escrow. Resources in the normal stockpiles are free for the AI to use, while resources in the escrow stockpiles can only be used with [up-build](/commands/commands-details.html#up-build), [up-train](/commands/commands-details.html#up-train), or [up-research](/commands/commands-details.html#up-research) if the EscrowGoalId parameter in these commands is a goal set to the value "with-escrow". The user interface shows the sum of both the normal and escrow stockpile resources added together for each resource.

By default, all resources are stored in the normal stockpiles. However, [set-escrow-percentage](/commands/commands-details.html#set-escrow-percentage) and [up-modify-escrow](/commands/commands-details.html#up-modify-escrow) can be used to store some or all of the AI's resources in the escrow stockpiles instead. Resources in the escrow stockpiles can transferred back into the normal stockpiles by using release-escrow, [up-release-escrow](/commands/commands-details.html#up-release-escrow), or [up-modify-escrow](/commands/commands-details.html#up-modify-escrow).

Resources are usually placed in escrow stockpiles in order to save up for expensive technologies or important buildings or units, so that it isn't spent on lower priority things.

**参数**：`Resource`

*示例：Release the AI's escrow food and gold so that there is enough food and gold available in the normal stockpiles to research Imperial Age.*
```
(defrule
	(can-research-with-escrow imperial-age)
=>
	(release-escrow food)
	(release-escrow gold)
	(research imperial-age)
)
```

相关命令：`escrow-amount`, `set-escrow-percentage`, `up-modify-escrow`, `up-release-escrow`

### `research`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Techs · 复杂度 Low*

**Researches the given item.**

Researches the given item if the technology is available to the player and the technology can be researched without escrowed resources. Please use [can-research](/commands/commands-details.html#can-research), [can-research-with-escrow](/commands/commands-details.html#can-research-with-escrow), or [up-can-research](/commands/commands-details.html#up-can-research) in any rule where you use the research command, in order to prevent possible crashes.

To prevent cheating, this action will fail if the item currently cannot be researched (i.e. the tech prerequisites are not met, there is no available building, or the player cannot afford the item).

Research names, except for ages, my-unique-research, my-second-unique-research, are prefixed with a "ri-" which might stand for "research item". You can also research by the research ID rather than the research name. You can see all technologies and their research IDs in the [Technologies table](/tables/techs.html). You can also use my-unique-research, which will usually (always in DE) research the imperial age unique tech for the civilization, and you can also use my-second-unique-research, which will usually (always in DE) research the castle age unique tech for the civilization. In UP and WK, the exceptions are the Britons (in WK only) and Goths, whose my-unique-research and my-second-unique-research are switched.

**参数**：`TechId`

*示例：If playing as the Mayans, research the Crossbowman upgrade.*
```
(defrule
	(civ-selected mayan)
	(can-research ri-crossbow)
=>
	(research ri-crossbow)
)
```

相关命令：`can-afford-research`, `can-research`, `can-research-with-escrow`, `research-available`, `research-completed`, `up-can-research`, `up-research`, `up-research-status`

相关 SN：`sn-enable-research-queue`, `sn-enable-training-queue`

### `resign`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Other · 复杂度 Low*

**Causes the computer player to resign.**

*示例：Resign when we have less than 2 population and no town center.*
```
(defrule
	(population < 2)
	(building-type-count-total town-center == 0)
=>
	(chat-to-all "gg")
	(resign)
)
```

相关命令：`player-in-game`, `player-resigned`, `up-players-in-game`

### `sell-commodity`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Economy, Trading · 复杂度 Low*

**Sells one lot of a given commodity.**

Sells one lot of a given commodity. The AI will sell 100 of the given commodity (wood, food, or stone) in return for gold at the current [commodity-selling-price](/commands/commands-details.html#commodity-selling-price). The commodity selling price is the amount of gold that will be added to the gold stockpile when 100 of the specified commodity (wood, food, or stone) is sold. This price can range between 14 and infinity without Guilds, between 17 and infinity with Guilds, and between 19 and infinity when playing Saracens.

**参数**：`Commodity`

*示例：If we have more than 600 food and less than 100 gold, sell food if the commodity-selling-price is at least 30.*
```
(defrule
	(food-amount > 600)
	(gold-amount < 100)
	(commodity-selling-price >= 30)
	(can-sell-commodity food)
=>
	(sell-commodity food)
)
```

相关命令：`buy-commodity`, `can-buy-commodity`, `can-sell-commodity`, `commodity-buying-price`, `commodity-selling-price`, `up-buy-commodity`, `up-sell-commodity`

**The game does not use it for anything.**

### `set-author-name`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Don't Use · 复杂度 Don't Use*

**The game does not use it for anything.**

相关命令：`set-author-email`

### `set-author-version`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Don't Use · 复杂度 Don't Use*

**The game does not use it for anything.**
