### `up-set-offense-priority`
*版本 UP · 分类 Attack · 复杂度 Medium*

**Set the offensive targeting priority for an object.**

Set the offensive targeting priority for an object. This is used when attacking with [sn-number-attack-groups](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-number-attack-groups) or [attack-now](/commands/commands-details.html#attack-now). [sn-enable-offensive-priority](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-enable-offensive-priority) must be set to 1 for these priorities to take effect.

Note: offensive priorities have a very small range. You can turn the priorities up to 11 (highest), but no more.

Also, unit lines do not work here, so just set the base unit type id (spearman for the entire spearman-line, etc.). Classes may be used, as well. If a unit has its type priority set, that will override its class priority.

Target units on -1 offensive priority will not hold the attention of attackers if a higher priority unit appears. If the target unit is not -1 priority, the attacker may retarget to other units nearby, but primarily to other units with the same offensive priority. Battering rams and cannon galleons prefer to attack non-moving targets, while all other units prefer moving targets. If you clear offensive priorities with [up-reset-target-priorities](/commands/commands-details.html#up-reset-target-priorities), you may notice attack behavior that is a bit similar to patrol.

Default offensive priorities by class/type id: (classes that are not actually used in the game are marked with an asterisk)

PriorityClass/Unit IDClass/Unit Name-1default0903, 927, 939, 949, 951, 952, 954building-class, wall-class, gate-class, farm-class, packed-trebuchet-class, tower-class, unpacked-trebuchet-class1906, 912, 924*, 935, 945*, 947, 950*, 956*, 957*infantry-class, cavalry-class, petard-class, scout-cavalry-class2928*, 946*3900, 923, 926*, 936, 944archery-class, cavalry-cannon-class, cavalry-archer-class, archery-cannon-class46, 7skirmisher, elite-skirmisher535, 422, 548battering-ram, capped-ram, siege-ram6913, 955siege-weapon-class, scorpion-class7904, 919, 958, 959villager-class, trade-cart-class, livestock-class, king-class8917*, 918, 943monastery-class, monk-with-relic-class9922, 953*warship-class10920transport-ship-class11902, 921trade-cog-class, fishing-ship-class

**参数**：`typeOp` — `ObjectId` — `typeOp` — `Value`

*示例：Increase the offensive priority for mining camps to 11.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-set-offense-priority c: mining-camp c: 11)
	(disable-self)
)
```

### `up-set-placement-data`
*版本 UP · 分类 Buildings · 复杂度 High*

**Specify placement information for managed construction.**

Specify placement information for managed construction. Please ensure Player has at least a town-center to use for reference, if they don't have ObjectId. If Player has no objects left, placement will not work as expected.

The properties assigned by up-set-placement-data that are in effect when a build command is executed are stored with them, so you can change properties immediately afterward and it won't break your previous settings. The action only allows exact player numbers, "my-player-number", or "this-any" rule variables for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber), such as "this-any-ally" or "this-any-computer-ally". It does not allow "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It cannot be used with players who aren't allies. It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `ObjectId` — `typeOp` — `Value`

*示例：Place 25 tiles behind the home town center.*
```
(defrule
	(up-can-build 0 c: house)
=>
	(up-set-placement-data my-player-number -1 c: -25) ; home town center = -1
	(up-build place-control 0 c: house)
)
```

*示例：Place 2 tiles in front of the focus-player's latest lumber-camp.*
```
(defrule
	(up-can-build 0 c: house)
=>
	(up-set-placement-data focus-player lumber-camp c: 2)
	(up-build place-control 0 c: house)
)
```

### `up-set-precise-target-point`
*版本 UP · 分类 DUC · 复杂度 Very High*

**Set the target point with an unchecked extended goal pair.**

Set the target point with an unchecked extended goal pair. This command is identical to [up-set-target-point](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-target-point), except it will not bound the point inside the map. Please ensure the point is valid with [up-bound-precise-point](/commands/commands-details.html#up-bound-precise-point). A precise point is expected to be a normal point x100 for 2 places of decimal precision.

**参数**：`Point`

*示例：Set the target point to the precise map center.*
```
(defconst gl-point-x 100)
(defconst gl-point-y 101)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-get-point position-center gl-point-x)
	(up-modify-goal gl-point-x c:* 100)
	(up-modify-goal gl-point-y c:* 100)
	(up-set-precise-target-point gl-point-x)
)
```

*版本 UP · 分类 Goals, Other Player Info · 复杂度 Medium*

**Set the value of a shared goal.**

**参数**：`typeOp` — `SharedGoalId` — `typeOp` — `Value`

*示例：Set the value of shared goal id 1 to 0.*

```
(defconst gl-value 100)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-set-shared-goal c: 1 c: 0)
)
```

### `up-set-signal`
*版本 UP · 分类 Scenarios · 复杂度 Medium*

**Set the value of a scenario trigger signal.**

Set the value of a scenario trigger signal.

This action only works with a single player scenario and "AI Signal" trigger condition. For a multiplayer scenario, use "Multiplayer AI Signal" and [fe-set-signal](/commands/commands-details.html#fe-set-signal).

**参数**：`typeOp` — `SignalId` — `typeOp` — `Value`

*示例：Set the value of signal id 1 to 0.*
```
(defconst gl-value 100)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-set-signal c: 1 c: 0)
)
```

相关命令：`acknowledge-event`, `event-detected`, `set-signal`, `up-get-event`, `up-get-signal`, `up-set-event`, `fe-set-signal`

### `up-set-target-point`
*版本 UP · 分类 DUC, Points · 复杂度 High*

**Set the target point for other commands with an extended goal pair.**

Set the target point for other commands with an extended goal pair. This command will also safely bound the point inside the map.

**参数**：`Point`

*示例：Set the target point to the map center.*

```
(defconst gl-point-x 100)
(defconst gl-point-y 101)

=>

)
```

*版本 UP · 分类 Timers · 复杂度 Medium*

Disable or enable a timer by interval. Set Value to -1 to disable the timer. If Value is positive, this will perform like the enable-timer action.

**参数**：`typeOp` — `TimerId` — `typeOp` — `Value`

*示例：Enable timer 20 to trigger in 10 seconds.*

```
=>
```

### `up-setup-cost-data`
*版本 UP · 分类 Cost Data · 复杂度 High*

**Set the goals to store cost data for food, wood, stone, and gold.**

Set the goals to store cost data for food, wood, stone, and gold. If the Option parameter is set to 1 the values of the provided cost goal set will be reset to 0.

**参数**：`Option` — `GoalId`

*示例：Set gl-cost-food, gl-cost-wood, gl-cost-stone, and gl-cost-gold to store cost data and initialize them to 0.*
```
(defconst gl-cost-food 101)
(defconst gl-cost-wood 102)
(defconst gl-cost-stone 103)
(defconst gl-cost-gold 104)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-setup-cost-data 1 gl-cost-food)
	(disable-self)
)
```

### `up-store-map-name`
*版本 UP · 分类 Game Info, Text Data · 复杂度 High*

**Store the current map name in the internal buffer.**

Store the current map name in the internal buffer. For rms, this is the filename of the map. However, if the map is a dynamic loader, such as Full Random, Random Land Map, or Blind Random, this will be the loader name instead of the actual map name. For scenarios, this will be the original save filename instead of the current filename. The buffer can be referenced by the chat-data commands using %s instead of %d with c: 7031232 (7031232 cannot be stored in a defconst). This buffer is shared by all AIs, so please store data before using it in a rule pass.

If the Option parameter is set to 1, the map name will be stored with the file extension in the name. If the Option parameter is set to 0, the map name will be stored without the file extension in the name.

**参数**：`Option`

*示例：Store the current map name in the buffer.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-store-map-name 0)
	(up-chat-data-to-all "Map: %s" c: 7031232)
)
```

### `up-store-object-name`
*版本 UP · 分类 Buildings, Text Data, Units · 复杂度 High*

**Store the target object's type name in the internal buffer.**

Store the target object's type name in the internal buffer. The buffer can be referenced by the chat-data commands using %s instead of %d with c: 7031232 (7031232 cannot be stored in a defconst). This buffer is shared by all AIs, so please store data before using it in a rule pass.

*示例：Store the type name for object id 0 in the buffer.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-set-target-by-id c: 0)
	(up-store-object-name)
	(up-chat-data-to-all "Object: %s" c: 7031232)
)
```

### `up-store-player-chat`
*版本 UP · 分类 Chat, Text Data · 复杂度 High*

**Store a player chat message in the internal buffer.**

Store a player chat message in the internal buffer. Note that only the last word of a chat message will be stored in the buffer and the message must be present in the host's chat history log (the PageUp key can find it). The buffer can be referenced by the chat-data commands using %s instead of %d with c: 7031232 (7031232 cannot be stored in a defconst). This buffer is shared by all AIs, so please store data before using it in a rule pass. The action only allows for exact player numbers, "my-player-number", or "this-any" rule variables for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber), such as "this-any-ally" or "this-any-enemy". It does not allow "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber`

*示例：Store the focus-player's chat in the buffer.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-store-player-chat focus-player)
	(up-chat-data-to-all "Message: %s" c: 7031232)
)
```

### `up-store-player-name`
*版本 UP · 分类 Other Player Info, Text Data · 复杂度 High*

**Store a player name in the internal buffer.**

Store a player name in the internal buffer. The buffer can be referenced by the chat-data commands using %s instead of %d with c: 7031232 (7031232 cannot be stored in a defconst). This buffer is shared by all AIs, so please store data before using it in a rule pass. The action only allows for exact player numbers, "my-player-number", or "this-any" rule variables for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber), such as "this-any-ally" or "this-any-enemy". It does not allow "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber`

*示例：Store the focus-player's name in the buffer.*
```

=>

```

### `up-store-tech-name`
*版本 UP · 分类 Techs, Text Data · 复杂度 High*

**Store a research tech name in the internal buffer.**

Store a research tech name in the internal buffer. The buffer can be referenced by the chat-data commands using %s instead of %d with c: 7031232 (7031232 cannot be stored in a defconst). This buffer is shared by all AIs, so please store data before using it in a rule pass. You can also use my-unique-research, which will usually get the imperial age unique tech for the civilization, and you can also use my-second-unique-research, which will usually get the castle age unique tech for the civilization. The excepts are the Britons, Franks, Goths, and Saracens, whose my-unique-research and my-second-unique-research are switched.

**参数**：`typeOp` — `TechId`

*示例：Store the unique research name in the buffer.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-store-tech-name c: my-unique-research)
	(up-chat-data-to-all "Tech: %s" c: 7031232)
)
```

*版本 UP · 分类 Text Data · 复杂度 High*

**Store a language string in the internal buffer.**

Store a language string in the internal buffer. The buffer can be referenced by the chat-data commands using %s instead of %d with c: 7031232 (7031232 cannot be stored in a defconst). This buffer is shared by all AIs, so please store data before using it in a rule pass.

**参数**：`typeOp` — `LanguageId`

*示例：Store 'Random' in the buffer.*

Store an object type name in the internal buffer. The buffer can be referenced by the chat-data commands using %s instead of %d with c: 7031232 (7031232 cannot be stored in a defconst). This buffer is shared by all AIs, so please store data before using it in a rule pass.

### `up-target-objects`
*版本 UP · 分类 DUC · 复杂度 Very High*

**Direct local search results against remote search results.**

Direct local search results against remote search results. The action-default command is equivalent to a right-click. This command can only perform the following actions: action-default, action-move, action-patrol, action-guard, action-follow, action-stop, action-ground, action-garrison, action-delete, action-gather, and action-none. The other [DUCAction](/parameters/parameters-details.html#DUCAction) options available for [up-target-point](/commands/commands-details.html#up-target-point) will not work. Set the Option parameter to 1 to target only the object set by [up-set-target-object](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-target-object). If set to 0, the objects in the local list will evenly target all objects in the remote list.

This command will aim to separate the units selected with [up-find-local](/commands/commands-details.html#up-find-local) into groups of 20 units or less before sending them against the remote target(s).

Do not use the action-default or action-move commands if the defensive targeting system (TSA) is locked on a target, or units will become "confused" and not respond for a few moments. Either bring the town size so that [enemy-buildings-in-town](/commands/commands-details.html#enemy-buildings-in-town) is no longer true or set [sn-disable-defend-groups](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-disable-defend-groups) on. The action-patrol command seems to work regardless.

**参数**：`Option` — `DUCAction` — `Formation` — `AttackStance`

*示例：Patrol selected units from the local search list against those from the remote search list.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-target-objects 0 action-patrol -1 -1)
)
```

### `up-target-point`
*版本 UP · 分类 DUC, Points · 复杂度 Very High*

**Direct local search results to a specific point on the map.**

Direct local search results to a specific point on the map. This command can perform all actions from the DUCAction list. However, action-default, action-guard, action-follow, and action-garrison will perform as action-move. If you wish to action-move back into formation nearby after attacking, please action-move to the point (-1,-1) first to reset distance.

This command will aim to separate the units in the local search list into groups of 20 units or less before sending them against the remote target(s). Do not use the action-default or action-move commands if the defensive targeting system (TSA) is locked on a target, or units will become "confused" and not respond for a few moments. Either bring the town size so that [enemy-buildings-in-town](/commands/commands-details.html#enemy-buildings-in-town) is no longer true or set [sn-disable-defend-groups](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-disable-defend-groups) on. The action-patrol command seems to work regardless.

**参数**：`Point` — `DUCAction` — `Formation` — `AttackStance`

*示例：Send selected units from the local search list to the map center.*
```
(defconst gl-point-x 100)
(defconst gl-point-y 101)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-get-point position-center gl-point-x)
	(up-target-point gl-point-x action-default -1 -1)
)
```

*示例：Train a spearman with action-train: (up-target-point EscrowGoalId action-train typeOp TypeId). Note: A goal ID is expected for EscrowGoalId to specify whether escrow should be used. However, if you never want this command to use escrow, you can put 0 instead.*
```
(defrule
	(up-find-local c: barracks c: 1)
=>
	(up-target-point 0 action-train c: spearman)
)
```

### `up-train`
*版本 UP · 分类 Units · 复杂度 Medium*

**Add a unit to the training queue with dynamic values.**

Add a unit to the training queue with dynamic values. You can also train unique units by using my-unique-unit, my-elite-unique-unit, and my-unique-unit-line, which will automatically get the UnitId of the unique unit, elite unique unit, or unique unit line that the AI's civ can train from the castle.

The setting of [sn-dock-training-filter](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-dock-training-filter) affects the ability for docks to train warships with this command.

**参数**：`EscrowGoalId` — `typeOp` — `UnitId`

*示例：Train spearman-line with escrowed resources, using a goal.*
```
(defconst gl-escrow-state 101)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(set-goal gl-escrow-state with-escrow)
	(disable-self)
)
(defrule
	(up-can-train gl-escrow-state c: spearman-line)
=>
	(up-train gl-escrow-state c: spearman-line)
)
```

相关命令：`can-afford-unit`, `can-train`, `can-train-with-escrow`, `train`, `unit-available`, `up-can-train`

### `up-tribute-to-player`
*版本 UP · 分类 Diplomacy · 复杂度 Medium*

**Tribute a variable amount of resources to other players.**

Tribute a variable amount of resources to other players.

The fact allows "focus-player", "target-player", and "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It also allows the use of rule variables for PlayerNumber, such as "this-any-ally" or "this-any-enemy". It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `ResourceType` — `typeOp` — `Value`

*示例：Send 100 food to the focus-player.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(set-strategic-number sn-focus-player-number 2)
	(up-tribute-to-player focus-player food c: 100)
)
```

### `up-ungarrison`
*版本 UP · 分类 Buildings · 复杂度 Medium*

**Request all objects of the specified type to ungarrison units.**

**参数**：`typeOp` — `ObjectId`

*示例：Release all garrisoned units in docks.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-ungarrison c: dock)
	(disable-self)
)
```

*示例：Release all garrisoned units in battering rams. Non-DE example.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-ungarrison c: battering-ram)
	(disable-self)
)
```

*示例：Release all garrisoned units in battering rams. In DE, you must use feudal-battering-ram instead since the base battering ram*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-ungarrison c: feudal-battering-ram)
	(disable-self)
)
```

### `up-update-targets`
*版本 UP · 分类 Attack, Defense · 复杂度 Low*

**Perform an immediate update for objects in town size.**

Perform an immediate update for objects in town size.

This command is important when using TSA. If you expand town size, new targets inside sn-maximum-town-size are quickly added into the target list (the list of enemy objects within sn-maximum-town-size). However, if you reduce sn-maximum-town-size, you have to wait until the target refresh for these objects to be removed from the target list, which happens every 15 seconds. This can cause issues with retreating, for example. Using up-update-targets will immediately update the target list, resolving the issue.

*示例：Retreat to the home town center after reducing town size.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(set-strategic-number sn-maximum-town-size 15)
	(up-update-targets)
	(up-retreat-now)
	(disable-self)
)
```


---

## Fact（162）

### `attack-soldier-count`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Attack, Counting · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's attack soldier count.**

Compares the computer player's attack soldier count to [Value](/parameters/parameters-details.html#Value) using [compareOp](/parameters/parameters-details.html#compareOp) and returns true if the condition is met. Attack soldiers are those attacking with the attack groups method (setting [sn-number-attack-groups](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-number-attack-groups) > 0) or are attacking with the [attack-now](/commands/commands-details.html#attack-now) command.

Setting sn-number-attack-groups to 0 and using [up-disband-group-type](/commands/commands-details.html#up-disband-group-type) to disband land attack groups when attacking with attack groups will reset the soldiers, and they will no longer be considered attack soldiers. Likewise, using [up-reset-attack-now](/commands/commands-details.html#up-reset-attack-now) when attacking with attack-now will reset the soldiers, and they will no longer be considered attack soldiers. Monks are included as land attack soldiers when attacking.

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check to see if we have at least 20 land soldiers attacking with attack-now or attack groups.*
```
(defrule
	(attack-soldier-count >= 20)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`attack-warboat-count`, `defend-soldier-count`, `defend-warboat-count`, `military-population`, `soldier-count`, `warboat-count`

相关 SN：`sn-number-attack-groups`, `sn-percent-attack-soldiers`

### `attack-warboat-count`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Attack, Counting · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's attack warboat count.**

Compares the computer player's attack warboat count to [Value](/parameters/parameters-details.html#Value) using [compareOp](/parameters/parameters-details.html#compareOp) and returns true if the condition is met. Attack warboats are those assigned to boat attack groups with the [attack-now](/commands/commands-details.html#attack-now) command, not with the [sn-number-boat-attack-groups](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-number-boat-attack-groups) SN. If you stop calling [attack-now](/commands/commands-details.html#attack-now) then they are immediately no longer attack warboats - even without using [up-reset-attack-now](/commands/commands-details.html#up-reset-attack-now).

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check to see if we have at least 5 warboats attacking with attack-now.*
```
(defrule
	(attack-warboat-count >= 5)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`attack-soldier-count`, `defend-soldier-count`, `defend-warboat-count`, `soldier-count`, `warboat-count`

相关 SN：`sn-number-boat-attack-groups`, `sn-percent-attack-boats`

### `building-available`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Can Do · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks that the building is available to the computer player's civ and that the tech tree prerequisites are met.**

Checks that the building is available to the computer player's civ and that the tech tree prerequisites are met. It does not check that there are enough resources to build the building. It allows the use of building line wildcard parameters for [BuildingId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#BuildingId). The only wildcard parameter available is watch-tower-line. However, it is better to use watch-tower instead of watch-tower-line, even after Guard Tower or Keep upgrades due to some bugs with watch-tower-line. Simply using (building-available watch-tower) will work regardless of tower upgrades. You cannot use building classes with this command.

When the AI checks the tech tree prerequisites, this includes checking whether the prerequisite age has been researched. There isn't a way at the beginning of the game to check if the building will be available for the civilization in future ages.

**参数**：`BuildingId`

*示例：Check if stables are available. This will return true if the AI is in Feudal Age, has a barracks, and isn't Aztecs, Incas, or Mayans.*
```
(defrule
	(building-available stable)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`can-afford-building`, `can-afford-complete-wall`, `can-build`, `can-build-gate`, `can-build-wall`, `research-available`, `unit-available`, `up-can-build`, `up-can-build-line`

### `building-count`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Counting · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's building count. Only existing buildings are included.**

Checks the computer player's building count. Only existing buildings are included, not buildings under construction. Buildings that existed from the start of the game, such as the starting town center, are not included. Also, farms are included, but walls and gates are not included.

To check for the building-count of other players, including buildings under construction, use [players-building-count](/commands/commands-details.html#players-building-count).

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the AI has at least five buildings, not including the starting town center, walls, or gates.*
```
(defrule
	(building-count >= 5)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`building-count-total`, `building-type-count`, `building-type-count-total`, `cc-players-building-count`, `players-building-count`, `up-object-type-count`, `up-object-type-count-total`

### `building-count-total`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Counting · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's total building count, either existing buildings or buildings under construction.**

Checks the computer player's total building count, either existing buildings or buildings under construction. Buildings that existed from the start of the game, such as the starting town center, are not included. Also, farms are included, but walls and gates are not included.

To check for the building-count of other players, including buildings under construction, use [players-building-count](/commands/commands-details.html#players-building-count).

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the AI has at least seven buildings, including buildings under construction but not including the starting town center, walls, or gates.*
```
(defrule
	(building-count-total >= 7)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`building-count`, `building-type-count`, `building-type-count-total`, `cc-players-building-count`, `players-building-count`, `up-object-type-count`, `up-object-type-count-total`

### `building-type-count`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Counting · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's building count. Only existing buildings of the given type or class are included.**

Checks the computer player's building count. Only existing buildings of the given type or class are included, not buildings under construction. To check the number of gates, use [gate-count](/commands/commands-details.html#gate-count) instead. building-type-count allows the use of building line wildcard parameters for [BuildingId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#BuildingId). The only wildcard parameter available is watch-tower-line. However, it is better to use watch-tower instead of watch-tower-line, even after Guard Tower or Keep upgrades due to some bugs with watch-tower-line. Simply using (building-type-count watch-tower) will work regardless of tower upgrades.

There are four ways you can specify the building "type":Building Name: the name of an individual building, such as house, watch-tower, or town-center.Building Id: the numerical ID assigned to each building, such as 12 (the barracks) or 70 (the house). See the ID column in the [Objects Table](https://airef.github.io/tables/objects.html) for a list.Building Line: the building line for the building. The only option here is watch-tower-line, and avoid using it as there are various bugs with it. Simply use watch-tower instead.Building Class: the class of a building, such as building-class, tower-class, or farm-class. Classes group several building types together into a single category. Using a building class will count all buildings of this class. See the Class column in the [Objects Table](https://airef.github.io/tables/objects.html) to see each building's class.

To check for the building-type-count of other players, including buildings under construction, use [players-building-type-count](/commands/commands-details.html#players-building-type-count).

**参数**：`BuildingId` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the AI has less than three fully constructed town centers.*
```
(defrule
	(building-type-count town-center < 3)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`building-count`, `building-count-total`, `building-type-count-total`, `cc-players-building-type-count`, `gate-count`, `up-object-type-count`, `up-object-type-count-total`

### `building-type-count-total`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Counting · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's total building count of the given type or class, either existing or being constructed.**

Checks the computer player's total building count. The total includes buildings of the given type class, both existing buildings and those under construction. To check the number of gates, use [gate-count](/commands/commands-details.html#gate-count) instead. building-type-count-total allows the use of building line wildcard parameters for [BuildingId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#BuildingId). The only wildcard parameter available is watch-tower-line. However, it is better to use watch-tower instead of watch-tower-line, even after Guard Tower or Keep upgrades due to some bugs with watch-tower-line. Simply using (building-type-count-total watch-tower) will work regardless of tower upgrades.

There are four ways you can specify the building "type":Building Name: the name of an individual building, such as house, watch-tower, or town-center.Building Id: the numerical ID assigned to each building, such as 12 (the barracks) or 70 (the house). See the ID column in the [Objects Table](https://airef.github.io/tables/objects.html) for a list.Building Line: the building line for the building. The only option here is watch-tower-line, and avoid using it as there are various bugs with it. Simply use watch-tower instead.Building Class: the class of a building, such as building-class, tower-class, or farm-class. Classes group several building types together into a single category. Using a building class will count all buildings of this class. See the Class column in the [Objects Table](https://airef.github.io/tables/objects.html) to see each building's class.

To check for the building-type-count of other players, including buildings under construction, use [players-building-type-count](/commands/commands-details.html#players-building-type-count).

**参数**：`BuildingId` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the AI has at least one lumber camp, either fully constructed or being constructed.*
```
(defrule
	(building-type-count-total lumber-camp >= 1)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`building-count`, `building-count-total`, `building-type-count`, `cc-players-building-type-count`, `gate-count`, `up-object-type-count`, `up-object-type-count-total`

### `can-afford-building`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Can Do · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks whether the computer player has enough resources to build the given building.**

Checks whether the computer player has enough resources to build the given building. It does not take into account resources in the escrow stockpiles, so it checks if the computer player can afford the building only with non-escrowed resources. It does not check that the tech tree prerequisites are met or if the building is allowed for the civ. It allows the use of building line wildcard parameters for [BuildingId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#BuildingId). The only wildcard parameter available is watch-tower-line. However, it is better to use watch-tower instead of watch-tower-line, even after Guard Tower or Keep upgrades due to some bugs with watch-tower-line. Simply using (can-afford-building watch-tower) will work regardless of tower upgrades. You cannot use building classes with this command.

**参数**：`BuildingId`

*示例：Checks if the AI can afford a wonder without escrow.*
```
(defrule
	(can-afford-building wonder)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`can-afford-complete-wall`, `can-afford-research`, `can-afford-unit`, `can-build`, `can-build-with-escrow`, `up-can-build`

### `can-afford-complete-wall`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Walls & Gates, Can Do · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks whether the computer player has enough resources to finish the wall at the given perimeter.**

Checks whether the computer player has enough resources to finish the given wall type at the [Perimeter](/parameters/parameters-details.html#Perimeter). Perimeter 1 is usually between 10 and 20 tiles from the starting Town Center. Perimeter 2 is usually between 18 and 30 tiles from the starting Town Center. If wall placement is enabled at a particular perimeter with enable-wall-placement, the AI engine will attempt to plan a roughly circular wall pattern within the given perimeter distances and construct the wall according to this pattern when the [build-wall](/commands/commands-details.html#build-wall) command is issued. In particular, can-afford-complete-wall checks:
The wall type is available to the computer player's civ.The tech tree prerequisites are met.Required resources are available.

It does not take into account escrowed resources, so it checks if the computer player can afford the wall only with non-escrowed resources. It does not check if wall area is explored or if [enable-wall-placement](/commands/commands-details.html#enable-wall-placement) has been used. [Perimeter](/parameters/parameters-details.html#Perimeter) is either: '1' for a 10-20 tile radius aroung home TC or '2' for an 18-30 tile radius.

**参数**：`Perimeter` — `WallId`

*示例：Checks if the AI can afford a stone wall at perimeter 2 without escrow.*
```
(defrule
	(can-afford-complete-wall 2 stone-wall-line)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`can-afford-building`, `can-afford-research`, `can-afford-unit`

### `can-afford-research`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Techs, Can Do · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks whether the computer player has enough resources to perform the given research.**

Checks whether the computer player has enough resources to perform the given research. The fact does not take into account escrowed resources, so it checks if the computer player can afford the unit only with non-escrowed resources.

In addition to checking if the computer player can afford the research, it also checks that the research is available for the civ, that it's not already researched, and that the computer player has reached the required age. Does not check if the required building is built.

You can also use my-unique-research, which will check the imperial age unique tech for the civilization, and you can also use my-second-unique-research, which will check the castle age unique tech for the civilization.

**参数**：`TechId`

*示例：Checks if the AI can afford the Paladin upgrade without escrow. If the player hasn't yet researched Cavalier, the civ doesn't have the Paladin upgrade available, or the player has already resarched Paladin, this will also return false, regardless of the player's stockpile.*
```
(defrule
	(can-afford-research ri-paladin)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`can-afford-building`, `can-afford-complete-wall`, `can-afford-unit`

### `can-afford-unit`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Units, Can Do · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks whether the computer player has enough resources to train the given unit.**

Checks whether the computer player has enough resources to train the given unit. Does not check anything else. The fact does not take into account escrowed resources, so it checks if the computer player can afford the unit only with non-escrowed resources.

The fact allows the use of unit line wildcard parameters for [UnitId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#UnitId). These wildcard parameters allow you to specify a unit line rather than an individual unit in the unit line. You cannot use unit classes with this command. my-unique-unit, my-elite-unique-unit, and my-unique-unit-line can also be used, which will automatically get the UnitId of the unique unit, elite unique unit, or unique unit line that the AI's civ can train from the castle.

**参数**：`UnitId`

*示例：Checks if the AI can afford a battering ram without escrow.*
```
(defrule
	(can-afford-unit battering-ram)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`can-afford-building`, `can-afford-complete-wall`, `can-afford-research`

### `can-build`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Can Do · 复杂度 Low*

**This fact checks whether the computer player can build the given building, not counting escrow stockpiles.**

This fact checks whether the computer player can build the given building. You cannot use building classes with this command. This command does not work with walls or gates. However, you can use [can-build-wall](/commands/commands-details.html#can-build-wall), [can-build-gate](/commands/commands-details.html#can-build-gate), [up-can-build-line](/commands/commands-details.html#up-can-build-line), or [up-can-build](/commands/commands-details.html#up-can-build) to check if walls or gates can be built.

In particular it checks:
It's available to the computer player's civ.Tech tree prerequisites are met (also works for the Khmer building prerequisites bonus).Resources needed for the building are available, not counting escrow stockpiles.

It does not check whether villagers exist to build it, or if there is adequate space for the building.

The fact allows the use of building line wildcard parameters for [BuildingId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#BuildingId). The only wildcard parameter available is watch-tower-line. However, it is better to use watch-tower instead of watch-tower-line, even after Guard Tower or Keep upgrades due to some bugs with watch-tower-line. Simply using (can-build watch-tower) will work regardless of tower upgrades.

Important Note: Always use a can-build, [can-build-with-escrow](/commands/commands-details.html#can-build-with-escrow), or [up-can-build](/commands/commands-details.html#up-can-build) condition in every rule where you use the [build](/commands/commands-details.html#build) or [up-build](/commands/commands-details.html#up-build) command. Without this condition, the building queue for this building may get stuck for the rest of the game.

**参数**：`BuildingId`

*示例：Checks if the AI can build an archery-range without escrowed wood.*
```
(defrule
	(can-build archery-range)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`building-available`, `can-build-with-escrow`, `can-build-gate`, `can-build-gate-with-escrow`, `can-build-wall`, `can-build-wall-with-escrow`, `build`, `up-build`, `up-build-line`, `up-can-build`, `up-can-build-line`

相关 SN：`sn-enable-new-building-system`, `sn-initial-exploration-required`

### `can-build-gate`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Can Do, Walls & Gates · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks whether construction of a gate as part of the given perimeter wall can start.**

Checks whether construction of a gate as part of the given perimeter wall can start. In non-DE versions, this command will only check if you can build stone gates. In DE, this command will check if you can build the gate type specified by [sn-gate-type-for-wall](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-gate-type-for-wall). Perimeter 1 is usually between 10 and 20 tiles from the starting Town Center. Perimeter 2 is usually between 18 and 30 tiles from the starting Town Center. If wall placement is enabled at a particular perimeter with enable-wall-placement, the AI engine will attempt to plan a roughly circular wall pattern within the given perimeter distances and construct the wall according to this pattern when the [build-wall](/commands/commands-details.html#build-wall) command is issued. Once the AI finds an appropriate location to build a gate within the given perimeter and the [build-gate](/commands/commands-details.html#build-gate) command is issued, the AI will replace four wall segments with a gate foundation.

can-build-gate checks:
It is available to the computer player's civ.Tech tree prerequisites are met.Required resources are available (not counting escrow resources).There is a location in an existing wall to build it.

It will return false if it cannot fit a gate 3 tiles away from existing gates.

In the DE version, to check if the AI can build palisade gates, set [sn-gate-type-for-wall](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-gate-type-for-wall) to 1 before using this command.

**参数**：`Perimeter`

*示例：Checks if the AI can build a gate as part of perimeter 1 without escrowed stone.*
```
(defrule
	(can-build-gate 1)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`building-available`, `can-build`, `can-build-with-escrow`, `can-build-gate-with-escrow`, `can-build-wall`, `can-build-wall-with-escrow`, `build-gate`, `enable-wall-placement`, `gate-count`, `up-can-build-line`

### `can-build-gate-with-escrow`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Can Do, Walls & Gates · 复杂度 Medium*

**Checks whether construction of a gate as part of the given perimeter wall can start, if escrow is used.**

Checks whether construction of a gate as part of the given perimeter wall can start. In non-DE versions, this command will only check if you can build stone gates. In DE, this command will check if you can build the gate type specified by [sn-gate-type-for-wall](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-gate-type-for-wall). Perimeter 1 is usually between 10 and 20 tiles from the starting Town Center. Perimeter 2 is usually between 18 and 30 tiles from the starting Town Center. If wall placement is enabled at a particular perimeter with enable-wall-placement, the AI engine will attempt to plan a roughly circular wall pattern within the given perimeter distances and construct the wall according to this pattern when the [build-wall](/commands/commands-details.html#build-wall) command is issued. Once the AI finds an appropriate location to build a gate within the given perimeter and the [build-gate](/commands/commands-details.html#build-gate) command is issued, the AI will replace four wall segments with a gate foundation.

 can-build-gate-with-escrow checks:
It is available to the computer player's civ.Tech tree prerequisites are met.Required resources are available including escrow stockpiles.There is a location in an existing wall to build it.

It will return false if it cannot fit a gate 3 tiles away from existing gates.

In the DE version, to check if the AI can build palisade gates, set [sn-gate-type-for-wall](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-gate-type-for-wall) to 1 before using this command.

**参数**：`Perimeter`

*示例：Checks if the AI can build a gate as part of perimeter 1, including escrowed stone.*
```
(defrule
	(can-build-gate-with-escrow 1)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`can-build`, `can-build-with-escrow`, `can-build-gate`, `can-build-wall`, `can-build-wall-with-escrow`, `build-gate`, `enable-wall-placement`, `gate-count`, `up-can-build-line`

### `can-build-wall`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Can Do, Walls & Gates · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks whether a given wall type can be built at the given perimeter.**

Checks whether a given wall type can be built at the given perimeter without escrowed resources. Perimeter 1 is usually between 10 and 20 tiles from the starting Town Center. Perimeter 2 is usually between 18 and 30 tiles from the starting Town Center. If wall placement is enabled at a particular perimeter with enable-wall-placement, the AI engine will attempt to plan a roughly circular wall pattern within the given perimeter distances and construct the wall according to this pattern when the [build-wall](/commands/commands-details.html#build-wall) command is issued.

In particular, can-build-wall checks:
The wall type is available to the computer player's civ.Tech tree prerequisites are met.There is a location to build a wall.Required resources are available, not counting escrow amounts.

This fact checks that there is enough stone for at least 5 wall pieces, whereas [can-afford-complete-wall](/commands/commands-details.html#can-afford-complete-wall) checks if there is enough stone for the entire wall.

The fact allows the use of wall line wildcard parameters for [WallId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#WallId). The only available wall line wildcard parameter is stone-wall-line. Note you are allowed to enable wall placement at both perimeters.

**参数**：`Perimeter` — `WallId`

*示例：Checks if the AI can build a palisade wall at perimeter 1.*
```
(defrule
	(can-build-wall 1 palisade-wall)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`building-available`, `can-build`, `can-build-with-escrow`, `can-build-gate`, `can-build-gate-with-escrow`, `can-build-wall-with-escrow`, `build-wall`, `enable-wall-placement`, `up-build-line`, `up-can-build-line`

### `can-build-wall-with-escrow`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Can Do, Walls & Gates · 复杂度 Medium*

**Checks whether a given wall type can be built at the given perimeter, if escrow is used.**

Checks whether a given wall type can be built at the given perimeter, including with escrowed resources.Perimeter 1 is usually between 10 and 20 tiles from the starting Town Center. Perimeter 2 is usually between 18 and 30 tiles from the starting Town Center. If wall placement is enabled at a particular perimeter with enable-wall-placement, the AI engine will attempt to plan a roughly circular wall pattern within the given perimeter distances and construct the wall according to this pattern when the [build-wall](/commands/commands-details.html#build-wall) command is issued.

In particular, can-build-wall-with-escrow checks:
The wall type is available to the computer player's civ.Tech tree prerequisites are met.There is a location to build a wall.Required resources are available including escrow stockpiles.

This fact checks that there is enough stone for at least 5 wall pieces, whereas [can-afford-complete-wall](/commands/commands-details.html#can-afford-complete-wall) checks if there is enough stone for the entire wall.

The Fact allows the use of wall line wildcard parameters for [WallId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#WallId). The only available wall line wildcard parameter is stone-wall-line. Note you are allowed to enable wall placement at both perimeters.

**参数**：`Perimeter` — `WallId`

*示例：Checks if the AI can build a stone wall at perimeter 2.*
```
(defrule
	(can-build-wall 2 stone-wall-line)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`can-build`, `can-build-with-escrow`, `can-build-gate`, `can-build-gate-with-escrow`, `can-build-wall`, `build-wall`, `enable-wall-placement`, `up-build-line`, `up-can-build-line`

### `can-build-with-escrow`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Can Do · 复杂度 Medium*

**This fact checks whether the computer player can build the given building if escrowed resources are included.**

This fact checks whether the computer player can build the given building if escrowed resources are included. You cannot use building classes with this command. This command does not work with walls or gates. However, you can use [can-build-wall-with-escrow](/commands/commands-details.html#can-build-wall-with-escrow), [can-build-gate-with-escrow](/commands/commands-details.html#can-build-gate-with-escrow), [up-can-build-line](/commands/commands-details.html#up-can-build-line), or [up-can-build](/commands/commands-details.html#up-can-build) to check if walls or gates can be built.

In particular it checks:
It's available to the computer player's civ.Tech tree prerequisites are met (also works for the Khmer building prerequisites bonus).Resources needed for the building are available including escrow stockpiles.

It does not check whether villagers exist to build it, or if there is adequate space for the building.

The fact allows the use of building line wildcard parameters for [BuildingId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#BuildingId). The only wildcard parameter available is watch-tower-line. However, it is better to use watch-tower instead of watch-tower-line, even after Guard Tower or Keep upgrades due to some bugs with watch-tower-line. Simply using (can-build-with-escrow watch-tower) will work regardless of tower upgrades.

Important Note: Always use a [can-build](/commands/commands-details.html#can-build),  can-build-with-escrow, or [up-can-build](/commands/commands-details.html#up-can-build) condition in every rule where you use the [build](/commands/commands-details.html#build) or [up-build](/commands/commands-details.html#up-build) command. Without this condition, the building queue for this building may get stuck for the rest of the game.

**参数**：`BuildingId`

*示例：Checks if the AI can build a town-center, including escrowed wood and stone.*
```
(defrule
	(can-build-with-escrow town-center)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `can-buy-commodity`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Can Do, Economy, Trading · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks whether the computer player can buy one lot of the given commodity.**

Checks whether the computer player can buy one lot (100 resources) of the given commodity (food, wood, or stone). The fact does not take into account escrowed resources. In other words, this checks if the AI has a market and enough gold at the current buying price for the specified commodity to be able to buy 100 of the specified commodity.

**参数**：`Commodity`

*示例：Checks to see if the AI has a market and enough gold to buy 100 wood.*
```
(defrule
	(can-buy-commodity wood)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`buy-commodity`, `can-sell-commodity`, `commodity-buying-price`, `commodity-selling-price`, `sell-commodity`, `up-buy-commodity`, `up-sell-commodity`

### `can-research`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Can Do, Techs · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks if the given research can start.**

Checks if the given research can start. In particular it checks:
The research item is available to the computer player's civ.Tech tree prerequisites are metRequired resources are available (not including escrow stockpiles).The appropriate building has no items in the queue so that it may start the research.

Research names, except for ages, my-unique-research, my-second-unique-research, are prefixed with a "ri-" which might stand for "research item". You can also research by the research ID rather than the research name. You can see all technologies and their research IDs in the [Technologies table](/tables/techs.html). You can also use my-unique-research, which will usually (always in DE) check the imperial age unique tech for the civilization, and you can also use my-second-unique-research, which will usually (always in DE) check the castle age unique tech for the civilization. In UP and WK, the exceptions are the Britons (in WK only) and Goths, whose my-unique-research and my-second-unique-research are switched.

**参数**：`TechId`

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can research Wheelbarrow (notice the unusual extra dash in the research name). If so, research Wheelbarrow.*
```
(defrule
	(can-research ri-wheel-barrow)
=>
	(research ri-wheel-barrow)
)
```

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can research Feudal Age. If so, research Feudal Age.*
```
(defrule
	(can-research feudal-age)
=>
	(research feudal-age)
)
```

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can research Wheelbarrow, but instead it checks by using the research ID (213). If so, research Wheelbarrow.*
```
(defrule
	(can-research 213)
=>
	(research 213)
)
```

相关命令：`can-afford-research`, `can-research-with-escrow`, `research`, `research-available`, `research-completed`, `up-can-research`, `up-research`, `up-research-status`

相关 SN：`sn-enable-research-queue`, `sn-enable-training-queue`

### `can-research-with-escrow`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Can Do, Techs · 复杂度 Medium*

**Checks if the given research can start, if escrow is used.**

Checks if the given research can start. In particular it checks:
The research item is available to the computer player's civ.Tech tree prerequisites are met.Required resources are available, including escrow stockpiles.The appropriate building has no items in the queue so that it may start the research.

Research names, except for ages, my-unique-research, my-second-unique-research, are prefixed with a "ri-" which might stand for "research item". You can also research by the research ID rather than the research name. You can see all technologies and their research IDs in the [Technologies table](/tables/techs.html). You can also use my-unique-research, which will usually check the imperial age unique tech for the civilization, and you can also use my-second-unique-research, which will usually check the castle age unique tech for the civilization. The excepts are the Britons, Franks, Goths, and Saracens, whose my-unique-research and my-second-unique-research are switched.

**参数**：`TechId`

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can research Wheelbarrow if escrowed resources are included (notice the unusual extra dash in the research name). If so, release escrowed food and wood and research Wheelbarrow.*
```
(defrule
	(can-research-with-escrow ri-wheel-barrow)
=>
	(release-escrow food)
	(release-escrow wood)
	(research ri-wheel-barrow)
)
```

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can research Feudal Age if escrowed resources are included. If so, release escrowed food and research Feudal Age*
```
(defrule
	(can-research-with-escrow feudal-age)
=>
	(release-escrow food)
	(research feudal-age)
)
```

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can research Wheelbarrow if escrowed resources are included, but instead it checks by using the research ID (213). If so, release escrowed food and wood and research Wheelbarrow.*
```
(defrule
	(can-research-with-escrow 213)
=>
	(release-escrow food)
	(release-escrow wood)
	(research 213)
)
```

相关命令：`can-afford-research`, `can-research`, `research`, `research-available`, `research-completed`, `up-can-research`, `up-research`, `up-research-status`

相关 SN：`sn-enable-research-queue`, `sn-enable-training-queue`

### `can-sell-commodity`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Can Do, Economy, Trading · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks whether the computer player can sell one lot of the given commodity.**

Checks whether the computer player can sell one lot (100 resources) of the given commodity (food, wood, or stone). The fact does not take into account escrowed resources. In other words, this checks if the AI has a market and has at least 100 of the specified commodity that it can sell for gold.

**参数**：`Commodity`

*示例：Checks to see if the AI has a market and 100 food that it can sell.*
```
(defrule
	(can-sell-commodity food)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`buy-commodity`, `can-buy-commodity`, `commodity-buying-price`, `commodity-selling-price`, `sell-commodity`, `up-buy-commodity`, `up-sell-commodity`

### `can-spy`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Can Do, Techs · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks if the AI can research Treason without escrowed resources. Only works in Regicide games.**

Checks if the AI can research Treason without escrowed resources. Only works in Regicide games. The computer player does see the revealed area around the enemy kings as expected. This command does not check if the AI can research Spies like you might expect.

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can research Treason without escrowed resources. If so, research Treason.*
```
(defrule
	(can-spy)
=>
	(spy)
)
```

相关命令：`can-spy-with-escrow`, `spy`

### `can-spy-with-escrow`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Can Do, Techs · 复杂度 Medium*

**Checks if the AI can research Treason, including escrowed resources. Only works in Regicide games.**

Checks if the AI can research Treason, including escrowed resources. The computer player does see the revealed area around the enemy kings as expected. This command does not check if the AI can research Spies with escrow like you might expect.

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can research Treason with escrowed resources. If so, release escrowed gold and research Treason.*
```
(defrule
	(can-spy-with-escrow)
=>
	(release-escrow gold)
	(spy)
)
```

相关命令：`can-spy`, `spy`

### `can-train`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Can Do, Units · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks that the training of a given unit can start.**

Checks that the training of a given unit can start. You cannot use unit classes with this command. my-unique-unit, my-elite-unique-unit, and my-unique-unit-line can also be used, which will automatically get the UnitId of the unique unit, elite unique unit, or unique unit line that the AI's civ can train from the castle. In particular it checks:
The unit is available to the computer player's civ.Tech tree prerequisites are met.Required resources are available (not counting escrow stockpiles).There is enough housing headroom for the unit.There is an appropriate building that is ready to queue or start training the unit, where the number of queued units and techs is less than the current [sn-enable-training-queue](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-enable-training-queue) setting.

The fact allows the use of unit line wildcard parameters for [UnitId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#UnitId), which means that you can use (can-train spearman-line), instead of (can-train spearman). Interestingly, you can safely use the base unit of a unit line with this command instead of the unit line version, and it will work regardless of any upgrades that have been researched. For example, you can safely use (can-train archer) even if Crossbowman has been researched. This capability is important if you are scripting for WololoKingdoms (WK) or any other mod where some unit lines aren't defined in the AI engine.

Unique units can be trained dynamically by using my-unique-unit or my-unique-unit-line as long as your aren't scripting for a Userpatch modpack like WK.

You can also train by the unit ID rather than the unit name. You can see all units and their unit IDs in the [Objects table](/tables/objects.html).

In WK, there are two units that use a separate placeholder unit ID for training purposes, and you must use it for all can-train, [can-train-with-escrow](/commands/commands-details.html#can-train-with-escrow), [train](/commands/commands-details.html#train), [up-can-train](/commands/commands-details.html#up-can-train), and [up-train](/commands/commands-details.html#up-train) commands. These units are the condottiero and genitour. Use ID 184 for condottiero-placeholder and use ID 732 for genitour-placeholder.

You cannot check for the ability to train units with unit classes (like infantry-class) or with sets (like huskarl-set, which includes castle huskarls and barracks huskarls). To check for units like huskarls or tarkans that can be trained at multiple buildings, you must each each unit type separately, such as (or (can-train huskarl) (can-train barracks-huskarl)). To check if mercenary kipchaks (elite kipchaks that allies can train after Cuman Mercenaries is researched) can be trained, use "mercenary-kipchak" rather than kipchak-line.

This fact will return false if the setting of [sn-dock-training-filter](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-dock-training-filter) currently restricts the training of ships.

**参数**：`UnitId`

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can train camels using the camel-line unit line. If so, train camels.*
```
(defrule
	(can-train camel-line)
=>
	(train camel)
)
```

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can train camels using the base unit. If so, train camels. Note, this example is equivalent to the example above.*
```
(defrule
	(can-train camel)
=>
	(train camel)
)
```

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can train camels using the camel unit ID (ID 329). If so, train camels using the camel unit ID.*
```
(defrule
	(can-train 329)
=>
	(train 329)
)
```

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can train its unique unit. If so, train the unique unit.*
```
(defrule
	(can-train my-unique-unit-line)
=>
	(train my-unique-unit-line)
)
```

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can train condottieri. If so, train condottieri. Condottieri, genitours, and mercenary kipchaks require a placeholder version of the unit in can-train and train commands.*
```
(defrule
	(can-train condottiero-placeholder)
=>
	(train condottiero-placeholder)
)
```

相关命令：`can-afford-unit`, `can-train-with-escrow`, `train`, `unit-available`, `up-can-train`, `up-train`

相关 SN：`sn-dock-training-filter`, `sn-enable-training-queue`

### `can-train-with-escrow`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Can Do, Units · 复杂度 Medium*

**Checks that the training of a given unit can start, if escrow is used.**

Checks that the training of a given unit can start. You cannot use unit classes with this command. my-unique-unit, my-elite-unique-unit, and my-unique-unit-line can also be used, which will automatically get the UnitId of the unique unit, elite unique unit, or unique unit line that the AI's civ can train from the castle. In particular it checks:
The unit is available to the computer player's civ.Tech tree prerequisites are met.Required resources are available including escrow stockpiles.There is enough housing headroom for the unit.There is an appropriate building that is not busy and is ready to start training the unit.

The fact allows the use of unit line wildcard parameters for [UnitId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#UnitId), which means that you can use (can-train-with-escrow spearman-line), instead of (can-train-with-escrow spearman). Interestingly, you can safely use the base unit of a unit line with this command instead of the unit line version, and it will work regardless of any upgrades that have been researched. For example, you can safely use (can-train-with-escrow archer) even if Crossbowman has been researched. This capability is important if you are scripting for WololoKingdoms (WK) or any other mod where some unit lines aren't defined in the AI engine.

Unique units can be trained dynamically by using my-unique-unit or my-unique-unit-line as long as your aren't scripting for a Userpatch modpack like WK.

You can also train by the unit ID rather than the unit name. You can see all units and their unit IDs in the [Objects table](/tables/objects.html).

In WK, there are two units that use a separate placeholder unit ID for training purposes, and you must use it for all can-train, [can-train-with-escrow](/commands/commands-details.html#can-train-with-escrow), [train](/commands/commands-details.html#train), [up-can-train](/commands/commands-details.html#up-can-train), and [up-train](/commands/commands-details.html#up-train) commands. These units are the condottiero and genitour. Use ID 184 for condottiero-placeholder and use ID 732 for genitour-placeholder.

You cannot check for the ability to train units with unit classes (like infantry-class) or with sets (like huskarl-set, which includes castle huskarls and barracks huskarls). To check for units like huskarls or tarkans that can be trained at multiple buildings, you must each each unit type separately, such as (or (can-train-with-escrow huskarl) (can-train-with-escrow barracks-huskarl)). To check if mercenary kipchaks (elite kipchaks that allies can train after Cuman Mercenaries is researched) can be trained, use "mercenary-kipchak" rather than kipchak-line.

This fact will return false if the setting of [sn-dock-training-filter](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-dock-training-filter) currently restricts the training of ships.

**参数**：`UnitId`

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can train camels using the base unit with escrow. If so, release food and gold escrow and train camels. Note, this example is equivalent to the example below.*
```
(defrule
	(can-train-with-escrow camel)
=>
	(release-escrow food)
	(release-escrow gold)
	(train camel)
)
```

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can train camels using the camel-line unit line with escrow. If so, release food and gold escrow and train camels.*
```
(defrule
	(can-train-with-escrow camel-line)
=>
	(release-escrow food)
	(release-escrow gold)
	(train camel)
)
```

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can train camels using the camel unit ID (ID 329) with escrow. If so, release food and gold escrow and train camels using the camel unit ID.*
```
(defrule
	(can-train-with-escrow 329)
=>
	(release-escrow food)
	(release-escrow gold)
	(train 329)
)
```

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can train its unique unit with escrow. If so, release all escrowed resources and train the unique unit.*
```
(defrule
	(can-train-with-escrow my-unique-unit-line)
=>
	(up-release-escrow)
	(train my-unique-unit-line)
)
```

*示例：Checks to see if the AI can train condottieri with escrow. If so, release food and gold escrow and train condottieri. Condottieri, genitours, and mercenary kipchaks require a placeholder version of the unit in can-train and train commands.*
```
(defrule
	(can-train-with-escrow condottiero-placeholder)
=>
	(release-escrow food)
	(release-escrow gold)
	(train condottiero-placeholder)
)
```

相关命令：`can-afford-unit`, `can-train`, `train`, `unit-available`, `up-can-train`, `up-train`

相关 SN：`sn-dock-training-filter`, `sn-enable-training-queue`

### `cc-players-building-count`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Cheat, Counting, Other Player Info · 复杂度 Low*

**A cheating version of players-building-count.**

A cheating version of [players-building-count](/commands/commands-details.html#players-building-count). This command works even if cheats are disabled. For use in scenarios only. The fact checks the given player's building count. Both existing buildings and buildings under construction are included regardless of whether they have been seen - fog is ignored. Unlike building-count, buildings that existed from the start of the game, such as the starting town center, are included. Also, farms are included, but walls and gates are not included.

The Fact allows "focus-player", "target-player", and "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or a human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Cheat through the fog of war to check if the every enemy has at least three buildings, not including walls or gates.*
```
(defrule
	(cc-players-building-count every-enemy >= 3)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`building-count`, `building-count-total`, `cc-players-building-type-count`, `cheats-enabled`, `players-building-count`

### `cc-players-building-type-count`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Cheat, Counting, Other Player Info · 复杂度 Low*

**A cheating version of players-building-type-count.**

A cheating version of [players-building-type-count](/commands/commands-details.html#players-building-type-count). This command works even if cheats are disabled. For use in scenarios only. This fact checks the given player's building count for the given building. Both existing buildings and buildings under construction of the given type are included regardless of whether they have been seen - fog is ignored.

The Fact allows "focus-player", "target-player", "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

It also allows the use of building line wildcard parameters for [BuildingId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#BuildingId). The only wildcard parameter available is watch-tower-line. However, it is better to use watch-tower instead of watch-tower-line, even after Guard Tower or Keep upgrades due to some bugs with watch-tower-line. Simply using (cc-players-building-type-count any-enemy watch-tower > 0) will work regardless of tower upgrades.

There are four ways you can specify the building "type":Building Name: the name of an individual building, such as house, watch-tower, or town-center.Building Id: the numerical ID assigned to each building, such as 12 (the barracks) or 70 (the house). See the ID column in the [Objects Table](https://airef.github.io/tables/objects.html) for a list.Building Line: the building line for the building. The only option here is watch-tower-line, and avoid using it as there are various bugs with it. Simply use watch-tower instead.Building Class: the class of a building, such as building-class, tower-class, or farm-class. Classes group several building types together into a single category. Using a building class will count all buildings of this class. See the Class column in the [Objects Table](https://airef.github.io/tables/objects.html) to see each building's class. Classes don't work for enemy players with players-building-type-count, but they do work with cc-players-building-type-count.

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `BuildingId` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Cheat through the fog of war to check if at least one enemy has a castle.*
```
(defrule
	(cc-players-building-type-count any-enemy castle > 0)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`building-type-count`, `building-type-count-total`, `cc-players-building-count`, `cheats-enabled`, `players-building-type-count`

### `cc-players-unit-count`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Cheat, Counting, Other Player Info, Units · 复杂度 Low*

**A cheating version of players-unit-count.**

A cheating version of [players-unit-count](/commands/commands-details.html#players-unit-count). This command works even if cheats are disabled. For use in scenarios only. This fact checks the given player's unit count. Only trained units are included and fog is ignored.

The Fact allows "focus-player", "target-player", and "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Cheat through the fog of war to check if an enemy has less than five units.*
```
(defrule
	(cc-players-unit-count any-enemy < 5)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`cc-players-unit-type-count`, `cheats-enabled`, `players-unit-count`, `unit-count`, `unit-count-total`

### `cc-players-unit-type-count`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Cheat, Counting, Other Player Info, Units · 复杂度 Low*

**A cheating version of players-unit-type-count.**

A cheating version of [players-unit-type-count](/commands/commands-details.html#players-unit-type-count). This command works even if cheats are disabled. For use in scenarios only, though most AI tournaments allows its use to see if particular Gaia objects are on the map at the beginning of the game, for custom map detection purposes. For example, some scripts will check to see if fish are on the map to detect if the map is a water map.

This fact checks the given player's unit count. Only trained units of the given type are included and fog is ignored.

The Fact allows "focus-player", "target-player", and "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1). Counting Gaia units (player number 0) is not considered cheating.

There are four ways you can specify the unit "type":Unit Name: the name of an individual unit, such as villager, spearman, or monk.Unit Id: the numerical ID assigned to each unit, such as 4 (the archer) or 74 (militiaman). See the ID column in the [Objects Table](https://airef.github.io/tables/objects.html) for a list.Unit Line: the unit line for the unit. This includes all units in a unit line. For example, archer-line includes archers, crossbowmen, and arbalests.Unit Class: the class of a unit, such as infantry-class, cavalry-archer-class, or monastery-class. Classes group several unit types together into a single category. Using a unit class will count all units of this class. See the Class column in the [Objects Table](https://airef.github.io/tables/objects.html) to see each unit's class. Classes don't work for enemy players with players-unit-type-count, but they do work with cc-players-unit-type-count.

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `UnitId` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Cheat through the fog of war to see if there are any fish on the map. Gaia is player number 0.*
```
(defrule
	(cc-players-unit-type-count 0 shore-fish-class > 0)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`cc-players-unit-count`, `cheats-enabled`, `players-unit-type-count`, `unit-type-count`, `unit-type-count-total`

### `cheats-enabled`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Cheat · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks whether the cheats have been enabled.**

Checks whether the cheats have been enabled. Cheating commands that start with "cc-" can be used by AI scripts even if cheats are disabled. This command specifically checks whether players can enter cheat codes in the chat.

*示例：If cheats are enabled and gold-amount is less than 1000, cheat "robin hood".*
```
(defrule
	(cheats-enabled)
	(gold-amount < 1000)
=>
	(up-cc-send-cheat "robin-hood")
)
```

相关命令：`cc-add-resource`, `cc-players-building-count`, `cc-players-building-type-count`, `cc-players-unit-count`, `cc-players-unit-type-count`, `up-cc-add-resource`, `up-cc-send-cheat`

### `civ-selected`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Own Player Info · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's civilization.**

Checks the computer player's civilization. You can use "my-civ," which will automatically detect the civilization the AI is playing as. Note that the civilization names used with this command for pre-DE civs are usually different than the civ's display name. They are like the [LoadIfSymbol](/parameters/parameters-details.html#LoadIfSymbol) civ names where they often use the adjective form of the civ name, not the plural name. See [Civ](/parameters/parameters-details.html#Civ) for a list of correct civ names to use with this command. You can also enclose code in a #load-if-defined [CIV-NAME]-CIV block if it should only run when a particular civ is selected.

To check for the civilization of other players, use [players-civ](/commands/commands-details.html#players-civ).

**参数**：`Civ`

*示例：Check if the AI is playing the Franks.*
```
(defrule
	(civ-selected frankish)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`players-civ`

### `civilian-population`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Counting, Units · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's civilian population.**

Checks the computer player's civilian population. The civilian population is villagers, trade units and fishing ships.

To check for the civilian-population of other players, use [players-civilian-population](/commands/commands-details.html#players-civilian-population).

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the AI has at least 100 civilian population.*
```
(defrule
	(civilian-population >= 100)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`military-population`, `players-civilian-population`, `population`

### `commodity-buying-price`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Economy, Trading · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the current buying price for the given commodity.**

Checks the current buying price for the given commodity. The current buying price is the amount of gold that will be deducted from the gold stockpile to buy 100 of the specified commodity (wood, food, or stone). This price can range between 26 and infinity without Guilds, between 25 and infinity with Guilds, and between 25 and infinity when playing Saracens.

**参数**：`Commodity` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Buy food if the current food buying price is below 200.*
```
(defrule
	(can-buy-commodity food)
	(commodity-buying-price food < 200)
=>
	(buy-commodity food)
)
```

相关命令：`buy-commodity`, `can-buy-commodity`, `can-sell-commodity`, `commodity-selling-price`, `sell-commodity`, `up-buy-commodity`, `up-sell-commodity`

### `commodity-selling-price`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Economy, Trading · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the current selling price for the given commodity.**

Checks the current selling price for the given commodity. The current selling price is the amount of gold that will be added to the gold stockpile when 100 of the specified commodity (wood, food, or stone) is sold. This price can range between 14 and infinity without Guilds, between 17 and infinity with Guilds, and between 19 and infinity when playing Saracens.

**参数**：`Commodity` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Sell wood if the current wood selling price is above 25.*
```
(defrule
	(can-sell-commodity wood)
	(commodity-selling-price wood > 25)
=>
	(sell-commodity wood)
)
```

相关命令：`buy-commodity`, `can-buy-commodity`, `can-sell-commodity`, `commodity-buying-price`, `sell-commodity`, `up-buy-commodity`, `up-sell-commodity`

### `current-age`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Own Player Info · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks computer player's current age.**

Checks computer player's current age. In Post-Imperial Age Start games, the current age is imperial-age, not post-imperial-age. To check for Post-Imperial Age Start, use #load-if-defined POST-IMPERIAL-AGE-START or [starting-age](/commands/commands-details.html#starting-age).

To check for the current-age of other players, use [players-current-age](/commands/commands-details.html#players-current-age).

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Age`

*示例：Check if the AI is in the Castle Age or the Imperial Age.*
```
(defrule
	(current-age >= castle-age)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`current-age-time`, `players-current-age`, `players-current-age-time`, `starting-age`

### `current-age-time`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Own Player Info · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's current age time (time spent in the current age).**

Checks the computer player's current age time (time spent in the current age). This time is measured in seconds.

To check for the current-age-time of other players, use [players-current-age-time](/commands/commands-details.html#players-current-age-time).

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the computer player has spent at least 5 minutes (300 seconds) in the current age.*
```
(defrule
	(current-age-time >= 300)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

相关命令：`current-age`, `game-time`, `players-current-age-time`

### `current-score`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Own Player Info · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's current score.**

Checks the computer player's current score.

To check for the current-score of other players, use [players-score](/commands/commands-details.html#players-score).

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the computer player's current score is over 9000.*
```
(defrule
	(current-score > 9000)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `death-match-game`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Game Info · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks if the game is a Death Match game.**

Checks if the game is a Death Match game. You can also enclose code in a #load-if-defined DEATH-MATCH-GAME block if it should only run in a death match game. In DE, ultra high and infinite resource random map games are considered death match games, and this command will be true in those games.

*示例：Check if the game is a Death Match Game. If so, set sn-cap-civilian-builders to 200.*
```
(defrule
	(death-match-game)
=>
	(set-strategic-number sn-cap-civilian-builders 200)
)
```

相关命令：`game-type`, `regicide-game`, `starting-resources`

### `defend-soldier-count`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Counting, Defense, Units · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's defend soldier count.**

Checks the computer player's defend soldier count. A defend soldier is a land-based military unit not assigned to attack groups. This includes soldiers attacking with attack-now, with sn-number-attack-groups set > 0, or with TSA.
