The fact allows "focus-player", "target-player", and "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `TauntId`

*示例：Launch an attack when an ally taunts 31 ("Attack an Enemy Now!") if the AI has enough soldiers to attack. Acknowledge the taunt so that the attack command is only sent once.*
```
(defrule
	(taunt-detected any-ally 31)
	(military-population >= 8)
=>
	(attack-now)
	(acknowledge-taunt this-any-ally 31)
)
```

*示例：If taunt 4 ("Wood please") is sent to the AI from an ally, we have more than 200 wood, and we have a market, then tribute 100 wood to the ally and acknowledge the taunt so that taunt-detected will not remain true during the next AI script pass.*
```
(defrule
	(taunt-detected any-ally 4)
	(wood-amount >= 200)
	(building-type-count market > 0)
=>
	(tribute-to-player this-any-ally wood 100)
	(acknowledge-taunt this-any-ally 4)
)
```

相关命令：`acknowledge-taunt`, `chat-to-player`, `chat-to-allies`, `taunt`, `taunt-using-range`

### `timer-triggered`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Timers · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks whether a given timer has triggered (the time on the timer has run out).**

Checks whether a given timer has triggered (the time on the timer has run out). For disabled or running timers this fact is always false. The check can be performed any number of times until the timer is explicitly disabled or enabled again (restarted).

The given timer ID can be any valid timer ID, which can range from 1 to 100 (1-50 in UP and 1-10 in 1.0c). You can also substitute a defconst that is defined with a value between 1 and 100 if you want to give the timer a name.

Timers have three possible states, and they cannot have multiple states at once: timer-running, timer-triggered, and timer-disabled. All 50 timers start in the timer-disabled state, and timer-triggered command is only true when the timer is in the timer-triggered state. To disable a timer, use [disable-timer](/commands/commands-details.html#disable-timer) or use [up-set-timer](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-timer) with a -1 timer length. To enable a timer, use [enable-timer](/commands/commands-details.html#enable-timer) or use [up-set-timer](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-timer) with a timer length > 0.

**参数**：`TimerId`

*示例：If the t-attack timer (defconsted as timer ID 31) is triggered, tell the AI to attack and disable the timer so that the timer is no longer in the timer-triggered state.*
```
(defconst t-attack 31)
(defrule
	(timer-triggered t-attack)
=>
	(attack-now)
	(disable-timer t-attack)
)
```

相关命令：`disable-timer`, `enable-timer`, `up-get-timer`, `up-set-timer`, `up-timer-status`

### `town-under-attack`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Defense · 复杂度 Low*

**Set to true when a computer player's town is under attack.**

town-under-attack is triggered (i.e. returns true) if any unit/building belonging to the computer player that is inside [sn-maximum-town-size](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-maximum-town-size) gets attacked. It lasts 1 to 10 in-game seconds after the attack. It is not triggered by attacks to buildings or villagers that are outside sn-maximum-town-size. This command detects ally attackers.

Because town-under-attack detects any attack events within sn-maximum-town-size, it can sometimes trigger town-under-attack in conditions when a human player wouldn't consider the town under attack, such as if a wolf attacks a villager or an enemy scout attacks a villager while exploring. Most importantly, town-under-attack can trigger when the AI is using TSA to attack the enemy, since sn-maximum-town-size is large enough to detect attack events that occur in the enemy's town, so use town-under-attack with care.

*示例：If the town is under attack, the enemy has at least 6 knights, and we have less than 2 monks, then train a monk if possible.*
```
(defrule
	(town-under-attack)
	(players-unit-type-count any-enemy knight-line >= 6)
	(unit-type-count-total monk < 2)
	(can-train monk)
=>
	(train monk)
)
```

相关命令：`enemy-buildings-in-town`, `up-attacker-class`, `up-building-type-in-town`, `up-enemy-buildings-in-town`, `up-enemy-units-in-town`, `up-enemy-villagers-in-town`, `up-find-player`, `up-get-attacker-class`, `up-get-projectile-player`, `up-get-threat-data`, `up-projectile-detected`, `up-projectile-target`, `up-unit-type-in-town`, `up-villager-type-in-town`

相关 SN：`sn-allow-civilian-defense`, `sn-allow-civilian-offense`, `sn-disable-defend-groups`, `sn-disable-villager-garrison`, `sn-maximum-town-size`, `sn-number-civilian-militia`, `sn-safe-town-size`

### `true`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Other · 复杂度 Low*

**A Fact that is always true.**

A Fact that is always true. Each rule has to have at least one fact/condition, so this command is often used as a placeholder for rules that should execute its actions without conditions.

*示例：.*

```
(defrule

=>

```

### `unit-available`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Can Do, Units · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks that the unit is available to the computer player's civ, and that the tech tree prerequisites for training the unit are met.**

Checks that the unit is available to the computer player's civ, and that the tech tree prerequisites for training the unit are met. The fact does not check whether the unit training can start, meaning this command does not check resource availability, housing headroom, or whether the building needed for training is currently used for research/training of another unit.

The fact allows the use of unit line wildcard parameters for [UnitId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#UnitId). my-unique-unit, my-elite-unique-unit, and my-unique-unit-line can also be used, which will automatically get the UnitId of the unique unit, elite unique unit, or unique unit line that the AI's civ can train from the castle.

When the AI checks the tech tree prerequisites, this includes checking whether the prerequisite age has been researched. There isn't a way at the beginning of the game to check if the unit will be available for the civilization in future ages.

**参数**：`UnitId`

*示例：If bombard cannons aren't available, train trebuchets if we have less than 5 of them. trebuchet-set counts both packed and unpacked trebuchets.*
```
(defrule
	(not	(unit-available bombard-cannon-line))
	(can-train trebuchet)
	(unit-type-count-total trebuchet-set < 5)
=>
	(train trebuchet)
)
```

相关命令：`building-available`, `can-afford-unit`, `can-train`, `research-available`, `unit-available`, `up-can-train`

Checks the computer player's unit count. Only trained units are included.

To check for the unit-count of other players, use [players-unit-count](/commands/commands-details.html#players-unit-count).

```
(defrule

=>

)
```

**Checks the computer player's total unit count. The total includes trained and queued units.**

Checks the computer player's total unit count. The total includes trained and queued units.

To check for the unit-count of other players (not including queued units), use [players-unit-type-count](/commands/commands-details.html#players-unit-type-count).

### `unit-type-count`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Counting, Units · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's unit count of the given type.**

Checks the computer player's unit count of the given type. Only trained units of the given type are included.

The fact allows the use of unit line wildcard parameters for [UnitId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#UnitId). my-unique-unit, my-elite-unique-unit, and my-unique-unit-line can also be used, which will automatically get the UnitId of the unique unit, elite unique unit, or unique unit line that the AI's civ can train from the castle.

There are four ways you can specify the unit "type":Unit Name: the name of an individual unit, such as villager, knight, or cavalry-archer.Unit Id: the numerical ID assigned to each unit, such as 4 (the archer) or 74 (the militia). See the ID column in the [Objects Table](https://airef.github.io/tables/objects.html) for a list.Unit Line: the unit line for the unit, such as spearman-line or militiaman-line. Unit lines include each upgrade type of each type of unit. For example, spearman-line includes spearmen, pikemen, and halberdiers.Unit Class: the class of a unit, such as infantry-class, cavalry-class, or scorpion-class. Classes group several unit types together into a single category. Using a unit class will count all units that belong to this class. See the Class column in the [Objects Table](https://airef.github.io/tables/objects.html) to see each units's class.

To check for the unit-type-count of other players, use [players-unit-type-count](/commands/commands-details.html#players-unit-type-count).

**参数**：`UnitId` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：If we have at least 24 villagers, research Loom.*
```
(defrule
	(unit-type-count villager >= 24)
	(can-research ri-loom)
=>
	(research ri-loom)
)
```

相关命令：`building-type-count`, `building-type-count-total`, `cc-players-unit-type-count`, `players-unit-type-count`, `unit-count`, `unit-count-total`, `unit-type-count-total`, `up-object-type-count`, `up-object-type-count-total`

### `unit-type-count-total`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Counting, Units · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's unit count of the given type, including queued units.**

Checks the computer player's unit count of the given type, including queued units.

The fact allows the use of unit line wildcard parameters for [UnitId](/parameters/parameters-details.html#UnitId). my-unique-unit, my-elite-unique-unit, and my-unique-unit-line can also be used, which will automatically get the UnitId of the unique unit, elite unique unit, or unique unit line that the AI's civ can train from the castle.

There are four ways you can specify the unit "type":Unit Name: the name of an individual unit, such as villager, knight, or cavalry-archer.Unit Id: the numerical ID assigned to each unit, such as 4 (the archer) or 74 (the militia). See the ID column in the [Objects Table](https://airef.github.io/tables/objects.html) for a list.Unit Line: the unit line for the unit, such as spearman-line or militiaman-line. Unit lines include each upgrade type of each type of unit. For example, spearman-line includes spearmen, pikemen, and halberdiers.Unit Class: the class of a unit, such as infantry-class, cavalry-class, or scorpion-class. Classes group several unit types together into a single category. Using a unit class will count all units that belong to this class. See the Class column in the [Objects Table](https://airef.github.io/tables/objects.html) to see each units's class.

To check for the unit-type-count of other players, use [players-unit-type-count](/commands/commands-details.html#players-unit-type-count).

**参数**：`UnitId` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：If we have less than 20 archer-line units, including archer-line units currently being trained, train more.*
```
(defrule
	(unit-type-count-total archer-line < 20)
	(can-train archer-line)
=>
	(train archer-line)
)
```

### `up-compare-const`
*版本 UP · 分类 Other · 复杂度 Medium*

**Perform a comparison with a constant value.**

Perform a comparison with a constant value. A defconst that defines a string (quoted text) stores a string table index where the string is stored. Therefore, up-compare-const will compare against the string index of such a defconst, rather than the text itself.

**参数**：`Defconst` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the const named "feudal-villagers" >= 20.*
```
(defconst feudal-villagers 30)
(defrule
	(up-compare-const feudal-villagers >= 20)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-compare-flag`
*版本 UP · 分类 Goals · 复杂度 High*

**Perform a bitwise flag test with a goal variable.**

Perform a bitwise flag test with a goal variable. Flags allow multiple states to be stored in a single value by using powers of 2 (1, 2, 4, 8, 16, etc.). You can use [cgs]:== to see if a flag is stored or [cgs]:!= to see if it isn't stored.

**参数**：`GoalId` — `compareOp` — `Flag`

*示例：Check if the goal named "gl-guard-flag" contains the flag for guard-flag-resource.*
```
(defconst gl-guard-flag 103)
(defrule
	(up-compare-flag gl-guard-flag == guard-flag-resource)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-compare-goal`
*版本 UP · 分类 Goals · 复杂度 Medium*

**Perform a comparison with a goal variable.**

**参数**：`GoalId` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the goal named "gl-sheep-total" stores a value < 4.*
```
(defconst gl-sheep-total 101)
(defrule
	(up-compare-goal gl-sheep-total < 4)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-compare-sn`
*版本 UP · 分类 SNs · 复杂度 Medium*

**Perform a comparison with a strategic number.**

**参数**：`GoalId` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the strategic number named "sn-maximum-town-size" > 40.*
```
(defrule
	(up-compare-sn sn-maximum-town-size > 40)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-compare-text`
*版本 UP · 分类 Text Data · 复杂度 High*

**Perform a string comparison with the stored text.**

Perform a string comparison with the stored text. You must store text before using this command and the provided Defconst must be a text defconst. If the provided string cannot be found anywhere in the stored text, the value will be -1. Otherwise, the value will be the index of the match.

**参数**：`typeOp` — `Defconst` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if player 1 has "one" in their name.*
```
(defconst text-name-one "one")
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-store-player-name 1)
)
(defrule
	(up-compare-text c: text-name-one >= 0)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-defender-count`
*版本 UP · 分类 Counting, Defense, Units · 复杂度 Low*

**Check the number of units actively defending in town.**

Check the number of units actively defending in town. With this command you can check to see if your TSA attack is actually actively targeting anything or if it's just idling. If, after expecting your new town-size to initiate a defensive attack, the response from this command is far less than expected for several consecutive turns, your target may be unreachable by the defensive targeting system (target has been walled for protection by one of their allies, etc.) and you may need to switch targets.

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the AI is actively defending its town with at least 10 units.*
```
(defrule
	(up-defender-count >= 10)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-enemy-buildings-in-town`
*版本 UP · 分类 Buildings, Counting, Defense · 复杂度 Medium*

**Check the number of targetable enemy buildings in town.**

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if there is an enemy building inside sn-maximum-town-size.*
```
(defrule
	(up-enemy-buildings-in-town > 0)
	(enemy-buildings-in-town) ; this is equivalent
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-enemy-units-in-town`
*版本 UP · 分类 Counting, Defense · 复杂度 Medium*

**Check the number of targetable enemy units in town.**

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if there are more than 5 enemy units, except villagers, inside sn-maximum-town-size.*
```
(defrule
	(up-enemy-units-in-town > 5)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-enemy-villagers-in-town`
*版本 UP · 分类 Counting, Defense, Units · 复杂度 Medium*

**Check the number of targetable enemy villagers in town.**

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if there are at least 2 enemy villagers inside sn-maximum-town-size.*
```
(defrule
	(up-enemy-villagers-in-town >= 2)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-gaia-type-count`
*版本 UP · 分类 Counting, Economy · 复杂度 Medium*

**Check the current sighted resource count from gaia.**

Check the current sighted resource count from gaia. This command may be relatively slow, since it must check the status of all discovered resources within the requested subset (food, wood, stone, or gold).

This command does not work with relics.

**参数**：`typeOp` — `Resource` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if at least 4 sighted gold mines still exist.*
```
(defrule
	(up-gaia-type-count c: gold >= 4)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

*示例：Check if more than 6 sighted sheep or turkeys remain.*
```
(defrule
	(up-gaia-type-count c: livestock-class > 6) ; livestock-class = 958
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-gaia-type-count-total`
*版本 UP · 分类 Counting, Economy · 复杂度 Medium*

**Check the total sighted resource count from gaia.**

Check the total sighted resource count from gaia. When checking food, wood, stone, or gold, this command operates very quickly. However, the required data does not exist for specific food types, including deer and sheep. As a fallback, it will redirect to the slower [up-gaia-type-count](/commands/commands-details.html#up-gaia-type-count), and the result will only reflect resources that still exist.

This command does not work with relics.

**参数**：`typeOp` — `Resource` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the AI has discovered over 100 trees, which may or may not still exist.*
```
(defrule
	(up-gaia-type-count-total c: wood > 100)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

*示例：Check if less than 2 sighted deer remain (redirect to up-gaia-type-count).*
```
(defrule
	(up-gaia-type-count-total c: deer < 2)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-path-distance`
*版本 UP · 分类 DUC, Points · 复杂度 Very High*

**Check the distance from the target object to a specified point goal pair.**

Check the distance from the target object to a specified point goal pair. The distance will be 65535 if the point is unreachable. Set the Option parameter to 1 to require an open destination tile to find the path distance toward or 0 to allow for a few tiles of separation to find a reachable open tile.

**参数**：`Point` — `Option` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the stored object can reach the specified point.*
```
(defconst gl-center-x 100)
(defconst gl-center-y 101)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-get-point position-center gl-center-x)
)
(defrule
	(up-path-distance gl-center-x 0 != 65535)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

```
=>
```

### `up-player-distance`
*版本 UP · 分类 Other Player Info · 复杂度 Medium*

**Check the distance in tiles to the nearest building of another player.**

Check the distance in tiles to the nearest building of another player.

The action allows "focus-player", "target-player", and "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It cannot be used with players who aren't allies. It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if player 3 is less than 50 tiles away.*
```
(defrule
	(up-player-distance 3 < 50)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

*示例：Check if the dynamic "focus-player" is within "gl-distance" tiles.*
```
(defconst gl-distance 101)
(defrule
	(up-player-distance focus-player g:< gl-distance)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

*示例：Check if any enemy is within 40 tiles.*
```
(defrule
	(up-player-distance any-enemy < 40)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-players-in-game`
*版本 UP · 分类 Diplomacy, Other Player Info · 复杂度 Medium*

**Check the number of active players in the game of the specified stance.**

Check the number of active players in the game of the specified stance. Players are considered allied with themselves, so "ally" will include the AI player itself.

**参数**：`PlayerStance` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if there are at least 2 allies with the AI for a team of 3.*
```
(defrule
	(up-players-in-game ally >= 3)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-point-contains`
*版本 UP · 分类 Buildings, Points, Units · 复杂度 High*

**Check if an object exists at a point goal pair position.**

Check if an object exists at a point goal pair position. Set Point to 0 to use the point that is stored by [up-set-target-point](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-target-point). Please note that when used with all-units-class (-1), this may capture unexpected objects like birds flying over a tile, terrain plants, etc.

Also, this action will work whether the point has been explored or not. Using up-point-contains on unexplored tiles is permitted for the purpose of map detection and can be a useful way to determine the layout of various maps, such as maps that have forests or gold in the middle, but for other purposes you must make sure that a tile has been explored before you can use up-point-contains on it. You can use [up-point-explored](/commands/commands-details.html#up-point-explored) to check if a point has been explored.

**参数**：`Point` — `typeOp` — `ObjectId`

*示例：Check if a tree exists at the map center.*
```
(defconst gl-center-x 100)
(defconst gl-center-y 101)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-get-point position-center gl-center-x)
)
(defrule
	(up-point-explored gl-center-x == explored-yes)
	(up-point-contains gl-center-x c: tree-class) ; tree-class = 915
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `up-point-distance`
*版本 UP · 分类 Points · 复杂度 High*

**Perform a distance check between two point goal pairs.**

Perform a distance check between two point goal pairs. Set Point2 to 0 to use the point that is stored by [up-set-target-point](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-target-point).

**参数**：`Point` — `Point` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the distance from the home town-center to the map center is greater than 5.*
```
(defconst gl-self-x 100)
(defconst gl-self-y 101)
(defconst gl-center-x 200)
(defconst gl-center-y 201)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-get-point position-self gl-self-x)
	(up-get-point position-center gl-center-x)
)
(defrule
	(up-point-distance gl-self-x gl-center-x > 5)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

```
=>
```

Check the amount of food remaining on the current boar. This data is only valid if the boar is lured with strategic numbers (not Direct Unit Control), while another boar is targetable and available to hunt. If this is not the case, it remains invalid (65535) to signify that this is the final boar.

### `up-train-site-ready`
*版本 UP · 分类 Buildings, Can Do · 复杂度 Medium*

**Check if a unit's training site is ready and available.**

Check if a unit's training site is ready and available. You can also check the train site of my-unique-unit, which will automatically check the train site of the UnitId of the unique unit that the AI's civ can train from the castle.

Important Note: Unit lines, negative unit IDs, or invalid unit Ids may result in a crash. Do not use unit lines or unit classes with this command. Please use the root unit type instead, such as using archer instead of archer-line, even if Crossbowman has been researched.

In most cases, the unit you use to test whether a train site is ready doesn't matter. However, for docks, the unit you choose to test is important. Trade cogs may be rejected by the dock if you use [sn-dock-training-filter](/strategic-numbers/sn-details.html#sn-dock-training-filter) and it hasn't found an allied dock. On the other hand, a military ship (galley works to test all of these) uses enemy ships/docks to determine if it is acceptable when that sn is in use. Fishing ships may also provide a different result sooner or later.

An alternative to this command is finding a building you want to check, setting it as the target object with [up-set-target-object](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-target-object) or [up-set-target-by-id](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-target-by-id) and using [up-get-object-data](/commands/commands-details.html#up-get-object-data) like this:
(up-get-object-data object-data-progress-type gl-data)

 If 0 is stored in gl-data, then the building is not training or researching, and it is ready to train units.

**参数**：`typeOp` — `UnitId`

*示例：Check if a dock is ready to train a fishing ship.*
```
(defrule
	(up-train-site-ready c: fishing-ship)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

### `victory-condition`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Game Info · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the game victory condition.**

Checks the game victory condition. The victory conditions can be standard, conquest, time-limit, score, or custom.

**参数**：`VictoryCondition`

*示例：If the game has a standard victory condition, build a wonder if possible.*

```
(defrule
```

### `wall-completed-percentage`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Walls & Gates · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the completion percentage for a given wall perimeter.**

Checks the completion percentage for a given wall perimeter. Trees and other destructible natural barriers are included and count as completed.

The given perimeter must have been enabled with [enable-wall-placement](/commands/commands-details.html#enable-wall-placement), and you should not check the completed percentage until the pass after the given wall perimeter has been enabled.

Allowed perimeter values are 1 and 2, with 1 being closer to the Town Center than 2. Perimeter 1 is usually between 10 and 20 tiles from the starting Town Center. Perimeter 2 is usually between 18 and 30 tiles from the starting Town Center.

Note: There are multiple cases where wall-completed-percentage equals 100 when you wouldn't expect:Maps with starting walls like Arena, Fortress, or Hideout.On island maps if there is an entirely water based barrier between the AI and any enemies.If a treaty is active.If playing a Wonder Race game where you're allied with everyone

**参数**：`Perimeter` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if the walls for perimeter 2 are 100% complete.*
```
(defrule
	(wall-completed-percentage 2 == 100)
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

*示例：Check if there is a hole in our wall at perimeter 1.*
```
(defrule
	(wall-completed-percentage 1 < 100)
	(wall-invisible-percentage 1 == 0)
=>
	(chat-to-all "found a hole in the wall")
)
```

相关命令：`build-wall`, `build-gate`, `can-afford-complete-wall`, `enable-wall-placement`, `wall-invisible-percentage`

### `wall-invisible-percentage`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Buildings, Walls & Gates · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks what percentage of the potential wall placement is covered with fog.**

Checks what percentage of the potential wall placement is covered with fog. If the invisible percentage is not equal to 0 we do not know if there is a hole or not. This is because the hidden tile(s) might have a tree(s). The given perimeter must have been enabled with [enable-wall-placement](/commands/commands-details.html#enable-wall-placement), and you should not check the invisible percentage until the pass after the given wall perimeter has been enabled.

Allowed perimeter values are 1 and 2, with 1 being closer to the Town Center than 2. Perimeter 1 is usually between 10 and 20 tiles from the starting Town Center. Perimeter 2 is usually between 18 and 30 tiles from the starting Town Center.

**参数**：`Perimeter` — `compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：If the invisible percentage for the wall perimeter is < 20% and we can afford to wall all of perimeter 2, build a wall at perimeter 2.*
```
(defrule
	(wall-invisible-percentage 2 < 20)
	(can-afford-complete-wall 2 stone-wall-line)
=>
	(build-wall 2 stone-wall-line)
)
```

*示例：Check if there is a hole in our wall at perimeter 1.*
```
(defrule
	(wall-completed-percentage 1 < 100)
	(wall-invisible-percentage 1 == 0)
=>
	(chat-to-all "found a hole in the wall")
)
```

相关命令：`build-wall`, `build-gate`, `can-afford-complete-wall`, `enable-wall-placement`, `wall-completed-percentage`

### `warboat-count`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Counting, Units · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's warboat count. A warboat is a boat capable of attacking.**

Checks the computer player's warboat count. A warboat is a ship capable of attacking. Fishing ships, transport ships, and trade cogs aren't included.

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：Check if we have at least 6 warships. If so, and we've found an enemy dock, attack.*
```
(defrule
	(warboat-count >= 6)
	(players-building-type-count any-enemy dock > 0)
=>
	(set-strategic-number sn-number-boat-attack-groups 3)
)
```

相关命令：`attack-soldier-count`, `attack-warboat-count`, `defend-soldier-count`, `defend-warboat-count`, `military-population`, `soldier-count`

### `wood-amount`
*版本 AoC · 分类 Economy · 复杂度 Low*

**Checks the computer player's wood amount.**

Checks a computer player's wood amount. This amount includes escrowed wood.

**参数**：`compareOp` — `Value`

*示例：If an ally doesn't have a town center, set this player as the focus player. If we have at least 500 wood and this ally has less than 275 wood, tribute them 100 wood.*
```
(defconst gl-ally-player 101)
(defrule
	(players-building-type-count any-ally == 0)
=>
	(up-get-player-fact this-any-ally player-number 0 gl-ally-player)
	(up-modify-sn sn-focus-player-number g:= gl-ally-player)
)

(defrule
	(players-building-type-count any-ally == 0)
	(wood-amount >= 500)
	(up-allied-resource-amount focus-player wood < 275)
=>
	(tribute-to-player focus-player wood 100)
)
```

相关命令：`food-amount`, `gold-amount`, `stone-amount`, `up-allied-resource-amount`, `up-resource-amount`


---

## Fact/Action（21）

### `up-add-object-by-id`
*版本 UP · 分类 DUC · 复杂度 Very High*

**Add an object to the search results by id.**

Add an object to the search results by id. This command can be used as either a Fact or an Action.

**参数**：`SearchSource` — `typeOp` — `Id`

*示例：Add an object to the local search results by a specific id.*
```
(defconst gl-stored-id 100)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-add-object-by-id search-local g: gl-stored-id)
)
```

### `up-find-local`
*版本 UP · 分类 DUC · 复杂度 Very High*

**Find objects owned by the local player for direct targeting.**

Find objects owned by the local player for direct targeting. If UnitId changes, the search index offset will be reset. Otherwise, it will continue from where it left off. This command can be used as either a Fact or an Action.

Note: this command ignores the object status filter set by [up-filter-status](/commands/commands-details.html#up-filter-status). up-find-local only finds objects with a status-ready status. To find objects of other statuses according to the status filtered by up-filter-status, use [up-find-status-local](/commands/commands-details.html#up-find-status-local).

**参数**：`typeOp` — `UnitId` — `typeOp` — `Value`

*示例：Find up to 20 infantry units owned by the player.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-find-local c: infantry-class c: 20) ; infantry-class = 906
)
```

### `up-find-remote`
*版本 UP · 分类 DUC · 复杂度 Very High*

**Find objects owned by the focus player for direct targeting.**

Find objects owned by the focus player for direct targeting. Set sn-focus-player-number before using this command. If the focus or UnitId changes, the search index offset will be reset. Otherwise, it will continue from where it left off. This command can be used as either a Fact or an Action.

Normally, up-find-remote will only find status-ready objects, but up-find-remote can also find objects with object status values 0 to 3 (status-pending, status-ready, and status-resource) if you search by object type id instead of class id.

The status filter set by [up-filter-status](/commands/commands-details.html#up-filter-status) is ignored by up-find-remote. To find objects of other statuses according to the status filtered by up-filter-status, use [up-find-status-remote](/commands/commands-details.html#up-find-status-remote).

For self/ally objects, it can find them directly at all times. For non-ally objects, if the object has been sighted and is either a building or has been seen/reseen within the past 5 seconds, it can be found. This should allow the AI to target units that are clearly visible without cheating, and target sighted enemy buildings in the fog.

One other note: although the new targeting and find commands aren't as heavy as attack-now, like any command that directly manipulates units like retreat-now, guard-unit, etc., please try not to flood them.

**参数**：`typeOp` — `UnitId` — `typeOp` — `Value`

*示例：Find up to 4 castles owned by the enemy, player 2.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(set-strategic-number sn-focus-player-number 2)
	(up-find-remote c: castle c: 4)
)
```

### `up-find-resource`
*版本 UP · 分类 DUC · 复杂度 Very High*

**Find gatherable resource objects for direct targeting.**

Find gatherable resource objects for direct targeting. This command stores data in the remote list and it will consider the status value set by [up-filter-status](/commands/commands-details.html#up-filter-status). To find stone, gold, fallen trees, and other directly gatherable resources, status-resource is required. For standing trees and living objects, status-ready is required. Please ensure the proper status is set before searching. The remote index will reset automatically when switching between this command and other remote search commands like [up-find-remote](/commands/commands-details.html#up-find-remote). If Resource changes, the search index offset will be reset. Otherwise, it will continue from where it left off. This command can be used as either a Fact or an Action.

When searching with boar-class (class 910), this command will not include wolves in the search.

**参数**：`typeOp` — `Resource` — `typeOp` — `Value`

*示例：Find 2 sighted gold mines and store them in remote results.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-full-reset-search)
	(up-filter-status c: status-resource c: list-active)
	(up-find-resource c: gold c: 2)
)
```

### `up-find-status-local`
*版本 UP · 分类 DUC · 复杂度 Very High*

**Find objects owned by the local player filtered by status.**

Find objects owned by the local player filtered by status. This is identical to [up-find-local](/commands/commands-details.html#up-find-local), except it will consider the status value set by [up-filter-status](/commands/commands-details.html#up-filter-status). If UnitId changes, the search index offset will be reset. Otherwise, it will continue from where it left off. This command can be used as either a Fact or an Action.

**参数**：`typeOp` — `UnitId` — `typeOp` — `Value`

*示例：Find up to 20 infantry units owned by the player.*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-filter-status c: status-ready c: list-active)
	(up-find-status-local c: infantry-class c: 20) ; infantry-class = 906
)
```

### `up-find-status-remote`
*版本 UP · 分类 DUC · 复杂度 Very High*

Find objects owned by the focus player for direct targeting. Set sn-focus-player-number before using this command. This is identical to [up-find-remote](/commands/commands-details.html#up-find-remote), except it will consider the status value set by [up-filter-status](/commands/commands-details.html#up-filter-status). If the focus or UnitId changes, the search index offset will be reset. Otherwise, it will continue from where it left off. This command can be used as either a Fact or an Action.

Read a fact for my-player-number into a goal. This command can be used as either a fact or an action.

### `up-get-fact-max`
*版本 UP · 分类 Other Player Info, Player Facts · 复杂度 High*

**Read the maximum value of the facts for specific players into a goal.**

Read the maximum value of the facts for specific players into a goal. This command can be used as either a fact or an action. The matching player will be set to the this-any-* rule variable for use in the action section of the rule, even if up-get-fact-max is used as an action.

The Action allows only the "any" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber), such as any-ally or any-enemy. It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `FactId` — `FactParameter` — `OutputGoalId`

*示例：Store the maximum ally score into gl-data.*
```
(defconst gl-data 101)

	(true)
=>
	(up-get-fact-max any-ally current-score 0 gl-data)
)
```

*版本 UP · 分类 Other Player Info, Player Facts · 复杂度 High*

Read the minimum value of the facts for specific players into a goal. This command can be used as either a fact or an action. The matching player will be set to the this-any-* wildcard player id for use in the action section of the rule, even if up-get-fact-min is used as an action.

The Action allows only the "any" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber), such as any-ally or any-enemy. It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `FactId` — `FactParameter` — `OutputGoalId`

*示例：Store the minimum enemy score into gl-data.*

```
)
```

### `up-get-player-fact`
*版本 UP · 分类 Other Player Info, Player Facts · 复杂度 High*

**Read a fact for a specific player into a goal.**

Read a fact for a specific player into a goal. This command can be used as either a fact or an action. For better performance, please use one of the more direct commands from the up-get-fact-* series whenever possible. The action only allows for exact player numbers, "my-player-number", or "this-any" rule variables for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber), such as this-any-ally or this-any-enemy. It does not allow "any"/"every" wildcard parameters for [PlayerNumber](/parameters/parameters-details.html#PlayerNumber). It also allows for scenario-player-# and lobby-player-#, where # is between 1 and 8. scenario-player-# refers to the player color (where red = scenario-player-2), and lobby-player-# refers to the player slot (where the lobby host or human player playing a single player campaign is always lobby-player-1).

**参数**：`PlayerNumber` — `FactId` — `FactParameter` — `OutputGoalId`

*示例：Store the civilian-population value for player 2 into gl-data.*
```
(defconst gl-data 101)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-get-player-fact 2 civilian-population 0 gl-data)
)
```

*示例：If an enemy has a castle, set this player as the focus player using the this-any-enemy rule variable.*
```
(defconst gl-enemy 102)
(defrule
	(players-building-type-count any-enemy castle > 0)
=>
	(up-get-player-fact this-any-enemy player-number 0 gl-enemy)
	(up-modify-sn sn-focus-player-number g:= gl-enemy)
)
```

### `up-get-point-contains`
*版本 UP · 分类 Points · 复杂度 High*

**Get the id if an object exists at a point goal pair position.**

Get the id if an object exists at a point goal pair position. Set Point to 0 to use the point that is stored by [up-set-target-point](/commands/commands-details.html#up-set-target-point). Please note that when used with all-units-class (-1), this may capture unexpected objects like birds flying over a tile, terrain plants, etc. This command can be used as either a Fact or an Action.

Also, this action will work whether the point has been explored or not. Using up-get-point-contains on unexplored tiles is permitted for the purpose of map detection and can be a useful way to determine the layout of various maps, such as maps that have forests or gold in the middle, but for other purposes you must make sure that a tile has been explored before you can use up-get-point-contains on it. You can use [up-point-explored](/commands/commands-details.html#up-point-explored) to check if a point has been explored.

**参数**：`Point` — `OutputGoalId` — `typeOp` — `ObjectId`

*示例：Get the id of a tree if it exists at the map center.*
```
(defconst gl-center-x 100)
(defconst gl-center-y 101)
(defconst gl-tree-id 102)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-get-point position-center gl-center-x)
)
(defrule
	(up-point-explored gl-center-x == explored-yes)
	(up-get-point-contains gl-center-x gl-tree-id c: tree-class) ; tree-class = 915
=>
	(do-nothing)
)
```

Read a fact for the target-player into a goal. This command can be used as either a fact or an action.

### `up-modify-goal`
*版本 UP · 分类 Goals · 复杂度 Medium*

**Perform math operations on the value stored in a goal variable.**

Perform math operations on the value stored in a goal variable. This command can be used as either a Fact or an Action, meaning the command can appear before the "=>" in the rule or after it. The behavior of the command is identical, regardless of whether it is used as a Fact or as an Action.

This command is a much more flexible version of the [set-goal](/commands/commands-details.html#set-goal) command, which only allows you to set a goal to a specific value. up-modify-goal allows you to add, subtract, multiply, divide, find remainders, find percentages, find min and max values, and do other mathematical operations, either with specific numbers or with the values currently stored in a goal or strategic number.

See the [mathOp](/parameters/parameters-details.html#mathOp) page for a full list of all the operations available, along with in-depth examples of each operation.

**参数**：`GoalId` — `mathOp` — `Value`

*示例：Add 1 to the value stored in "gl-sheep-total".*
```
(defconst gl-sheep-total 101)
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-modify-goal gl-sheep-total c:+ 1)
	(disable-self)
)
```

### `up-modify-sn`
*版本 UP · 分类 SNs · 复杂度 Medium*

**Perform math operations on a strategic number.**

Perform math operations on a strategic number. In DE, this command can be used as either a fact or an action, but it can only be used as an action in UP and WK. When used as a fact, it will modify the strategic number just like it would if it was used in the actions section of the rule. The only difference when up-modify-sn is used as a fact is that if it to modify the strategic number (because of an invalid strategic number ID or an invalid value), then the rest of the rule won't execute.

**参数**：`SnId` — `mathOp` — `Value`

*示例：Subtract 2 from "sn-maximum-town-size".*
```
(defrule
	(true)
=>
	(up-modify-sn sn-maximum-town-size c:- 2)
	(disable-self)
)
```
